Last push Nov 2025 — momentum slowing relative to [Rosetta](entities/rosetta.md). (2025-07-27 created)
- [so101_ros2 readthedocs (nimiCurtis/so101_ros2)](sources/so101-ros2-readthedocs.md) — MIT-licensed SO-101-specific ROS 2 workspace + LeRobot bridge (50 stars / 8 forks). ROS 2 **Humble** only; Python 3.10+; **8-package full workspace** (URDF/USD, hardware interface, controllers, bringup, teleop, bridge); **Isaac Sim 5.0+ integration** (unique among the 3 bridges); tested deployment of SmolVLA + π0.5. Dedicated readthedocs documentation site. Operational cost: 2 conda envs + dependency on the author's [`nimiCurtis/lerobot`](https://github.com/nimiCurtis/lerobot) fork rather than upstream LeRobot. (2025-06-12 created; v0.1.1 released 2025-12-13)
- [ros2-mcp-server GitHub (tanioklyce-dev)](sources/ros2-mcp-server-github.md) — first-party MIT skeleton exposing a ROS 2 robot's skills as [MCP](concepts/agents/llm-agent-architecture.md) tools for an LLM agent; the wiki's **first ROS 2↔MCP bridge** (agent↔ROS 2 tool-calling, a layer above the LeRobot↔ROS 2 bridges; calls [Rosetta](entities/rosetta.md) underneath). Config-driven tool filtering, structured `{status,reason,observation}` envelope, deterministic dispatch, out-of-band stop; `rclpy` lifecycle + fleet heartbeat wired, action/service calls still stubs. The [fleet framework](syntheses/projects/fleet-agentic-framework.md)'s Layer-2 bridge. **2026-07-05: AgenticROS-pattern layer added** — `blocks_base`/`interruptible` flags (enforced), `run_mission` templated step graphs, `compile_mission` no-LLM goal compiler, `robot_info` heartbeat + `find_robots_for` fleet layer, Zenoh RMW knob. (2026-07-04; updated 2026-07-05)
- [AgenticROS GitHub (agenticros/agenticros)](sources/agenticros-github.md) — community Apache-2.0 TypeScript ROS 2↔agent bridge covering **six platforms** ([OpenClaw](entities/openclaw.md) native plugin + one MCP server for Claude/Codex/[Hermes](entities/hermes-agent.md) + Gemini CLI + sandboxed [NemoClaw](entities/nemoclaw.md)); typed capability manifests (`interruptible`, `blocks_base`), mission step-graphs with `{{step.outputs}}` templating, deterministic NL→mission compiler, fleet primitives (`robot_info` heartbeat + `find_robots_for`), 4 transports incl. Zenoh, `/estop` bypassing the AI. Nav-first, **no manipulation/LeRobot path**; anonymous maintainers, 112★, no releases. Resolves the "is there a community ros-mcp-server yet?" open question. (2026; ingested 2026-07-05)
- [ROS 2 Humble docs](sources/ros2-humble-docs.md) — official documentation for the LTS distribution most commonly used as an integration target in robotics 2024–2026. Released 2022-05-23, EOL May 2027; 5-year LTS cadence; new ROS 2 distribution every May 23rd (World Turtle Day). Current distros: Lyrical Luth (May 2026), Kilted Kaiju (non-LTS), Jazzy Jalisco (LTS, May 2024 / EOL May 2029), Humble Hawksbill (LTS). Cross-distro communication not guaranteed. **Operationally load-bearing for LeRobot↔ROS 2 bridge selection** ([Rosetta](entities/rosetta.md) distro-agnostic; [lerobot-ros](entities/lerobot-ros.md) Jazzy-only; [so101-ros2](entities/so101-ros2.md) Humble-only).
- [NVIDIA RTX AI Garage — Hermes Agent on DGX Spark](sources/nvidia-rtx-ai-garage-hermes-agent.md) — Abhishek Gore, May 13 2026. Profiles [Hermes Agent](entities/hermes-agent.md) (Nous Research) on [DGX Spark](entities/dgx-spark.md); 140K+ stars in <3 months; "most used agent in the world according to OpenRouter"; pairs with Qwen 3.6 27B/35B; 4 distinguishing features (self-evolving skills, contained sub-agents, reliability by design, active orchestration). (2026-05-13)
- [Hermes Agent GitHub README](sources/hermes-agent-github.md) — `nousresearch/hermes-agent`; MIT; **171K stars / 28.7K forks / v0.14.0 (May 16 2026)**; Python 89% + TypeScript 8%; 7-layer architecture (Agent Core / Terminal Backends / Gateway / Skills / Tools / MCP / Memory); 6 terminal backends; 7+ messaging platforms; 200+ models via OpenRouter / Nous Portal / NIM / etc.; `hermes claw migrate` from OpenClaw. (2026)
- [OpenClaw GitHub README](sources/openclaw-github.md) — `github.com/openclaw/openclaw`; MIT; **375K stars / 78.3K forks**; TypeScript / Node 24; local-first personal AI assistant; 20+ messaging platforms; ClawHub skill registry; "Molty space lobster" naming. On the [ROSOrin Pro](entities/rosorin-pro.md), runs as upstream + Hiwonder's [`openclaw_controller`](entities/openclaw-controller.md) ROS 2 bridge module. (2026)
- [NVIDIA NemoClaw product page](sources/nvidia-nemoclaw-page.md) — NVIDIA's early-preview privacy/security wrapper over [OpenClaw](entities/openclaw.md); bundles NVIDIA Agent Toolkit + OpenShell policy guardrails + Nemotron local LLM; targets RTX PCs / RTX Pro / DGX Station / [DGX Spark](entities/dgx-spark.md). (2026)
- [A Collectivist, Economic Perspective on AI (Jordan, 2025)](sources/jordan-collectivist-economic-ai.md) — [Michael I. Jordan](entities/michael-i-jordan.md) essay (Inria/Berkeley; arXiv 2507.06268v3, cs.CY). AI's North Star should be a **market**, not "intelligence"; an LLM is a *collectivist artifact* (a culture, not a person). Proposes the **tripartite blend** of [computational + inferential + economic thinking](concepts/economics/three-thinking-styles.md); worked hooks: statistical contract theory (e-values ⟺ incentive-compatible contracts), three-layer data markets, [prediction-powered inference](concepts/economics/prediction-powered-inference.md). **Opens the wiki's economics-of-ML wing; no robotics content.** (2025-12-15)
- [Computational Life: How Self-replicating Programs Emerge from Simple Interaction](sources/computational-life-self-replicating-programs-paper.md) — [Agüera y Arcas](entities/blaise-aguera-y-arcas.md) et al. (Google Paradigms of Intelligence + U. Chicago; arXiv 2406.19108). Self-replicators **spontaneously arise** from random, self-modifying programs with **no fitness function** (BFF/Brainfuck, Forth, Z80, Intel 8080); sharp pre-life→life transition detected via novel "high-order entropy"; SUBLEQ a counterexample. **Opens the wiki's [artificial-life / emergence](concepts/alife/artificial-life-and-self-replication.md) wing.** (2024-06-27)
- [cubff (paradigms-of-intelligence/cubff)](sources/cubff-github.md) — official code behind [Computational Life](sources/computational-life-self-replicating-programs-paper.md); CUDA-or-CPU self-modifying program soup; substrates via `--lang` (BFF/Brainfuck variants, Forth, SUBLEQ/RSUBLEQ4). Apache-2.0, ~200★. **Disambiguates the paper's "BFF" from the unrelated `apankrat/bff` interpreter.** (2024)
- [BFF — Emergent Complexity experiment (Jonas Werner)](sources/jonas-werner-bff-emergent-complexity.md) — **independent from-scratch reproduction** of the [Computational Life](sources/computational-life-self-replicating-programs-paper.md) BFF soup (C+OpenMP + Python; 1,024×64-byte programs, 51.2M interactions/seed). Confirms spontaneous self-replicators + a sharp **"gelation" phase transition** (ops/interaction ~700→6,000–12,000, lineage diversity collapses) on a commodity desktop in minutes across multiple seeds. First independent reproduction of the result in the wiki. (2026-03-07)
- [Kriegman et al. 2020 — A scalable pipeline for designing reconfigurable organisms](sources/kriegman-2020-reconfigurable-organisms.md) — the original **[Xenobots](entities/xenobots.md)** paper (PNAS). [Evolutionary computation](concepts/alife/evolutionary-computation.md) co-designs body+behavior in a soft-body sim; designs built from frog (*Xenopus*) stem cells. Locomotion / object manipulation / transport / collective behavior / self-repair. The **biological** face of the wiki's ALife/self-replication branch. CC BY 4.0. (2020-01-13)
- [Blackiston et al. 2021 — A cellular platform for synthetic living machines](sources/blackiston-2021-cellular-platform-synthetic-living-machines.md) — the **cilia-driven "Xenobots 2.0"** paper (Science Robotics). Self-assembling *Xenopus* explants locomote via surface **cilia** (no sculpted muscle); emergent self-organization; the substrate for the self-replication study. **Gated — abstract-level ingest.** (2021-03-31)
- [Kriegman et al. 2021 — Kinematic self-replication in reconfigurable organisms](sources/kriegman-2021-kinematic-self-replication.md) — Xenobots **self-replicate kinematically** (swarms push loose cells into piles that mature into offspring); arises **spontaneously, un-selected**; AI-designed **C-shaped semitorus** triples replication rounds (~2→4). von Neumann replicators + exponential-utility forecast. PNAS; CC BY-NC-ND. (2021-11-29)
- [AI-Designed Living Robots Can Self-Replicate (IEEE EMBS feature)](sources/embs-xenobots-self-replicate-feature.md) — secondary journalism on the Xenobot self-replication result; researcher quotes (Blackiston/Kriegman/Bongard) + von Neumann framing + safety stance. (2022-03-12)
- [CRobots (troglobit/crobots)](sources/crobots-github.md) — classic **programming game** (Tom Poindexter, 1985; GPL-2.0 since 2013): C-programmed autonomous battle robots in an instruction-limited VM. Programming-game / autonomous-agent lineage (Core War family); hand-coded + static — no learning or replication. (1985; maintained)
- [Darwin (Bell Labs, 1961; McIlroy transcript)](sources/darwin-1961-bell-labs-game.md) — the **earliest digital-organism arena** and direct ancestor of [Core War](entities/core-war.md). Invented by Vyssotsky (Aug 1961); McIlroy coded the umpire; Morris's adaptive 44-cell species "won." Umpire-mediated `probe`/`kill`/`claim`; reproduction requires *relocation*; emergent population oscillations. The oldest entry in the wiki's self-replication lineage. (1961-08)
- [Dewdney 1984 — Core War (Scientific American)](sources/dewdney-1984-core-war-scientific-american.md) — the **founding [Core War](entities/core-war.md) text**; Redcode warriors battle in a circular core via the MARS VM; the **Imp** (`MOV 0 1`) is a one-instruction self-replicator. The self-replicating programming-game ancestor of the wiki's [Computational Life](sources/computational-life-self-replicating-programs-paper.md) thread. (1984-05)
- [pMARS — Portable Redcode Simulator (KOTH.org)](sources/pmars-koth.md) — the de-facto standard [Core War](entities/core-war.md) simulator (Ma/Sieben/Strack/Wangsaw, 1993); ICWS'94 draft + '88 mode; official rec.games.corewar simulator; KOTH email tournament hills. GPL-2.0. (1993-08-25) _fetch timed out; grounded via packaging metadata_
- [corewars.org — community hub](sources/corewars-org.md) — present-day [Core War](entities/core-war.md) landing page; definition, pMARS pointer, online hills, rec.games.corewars. Thin on technical depth. (rolling)
- [An Approach to the Synthesis of Life (Ray, 1991)](sources/ray-1991-tierra-synthesis-of-life.md) — the founding **[Tierra](entities/tierra.md)** paper (Artificial Life II). A hand-written 80-instruction self-replicator + mutation + reaper undergoes **open-ended evolution**; parasites, immunity, hyper-parasites, sociality, and cheaters **emerge spontaneously** (a digital Cambrian explosion). The evolved-replicator step between Core War and Computational Life. (1991)
- [Evolutionary Learning in the 2D ALife System 'Avida' (Adami & Brown, 1994)](sources/adami-brown-1994-avida.md) — the original **[Avida](entities/avida.md)** paper; tierra-inspired but **2D + local (cellular-automaton)**, which sustains more diversity (genotype-age D≈1.14 vs Tierra's 1.6). Demonstrates **evolving computation** (self-replicators evolve to add integers via task rewards). (1994-05-16)
- [The evolutionary origin of complex features (Lenski, Ofria, Pennock & Adami, 2003)](sources/lenski-2003-evolutionary-origin-complex-features.md) — *Nature*; the famous **[Avida](entities/avida.md)** result. A complex logic function (EQU) evolves from a replicate-only ancestor **only when simpler functions are also rewarded** — **0/50** populations evolve EQU when only EQU is rewarded vs **23/50** reward-all; deleterious mutations act as stepping-stones. The evolution-of-complexity capstone of the digital-evolution lineage. (2003-05-08)
- [Neural Cellular Automata: From Cells to Pixels (Pajouheshgar et al., 2025)](sources/pajouheshgar-2025-nca-cells-to-pixels.md) — high-resolution, real-time **[NCA](concepts/alife/neural-cellular-automata.md)** by decoupling coarse-grid dynamics from an implicit appearance decoder. Co-authored by **[Mordvintsev](entities/alexander-mordvintsev.md)** (NCA originator + [Computational Life](sources/computational-life-self-replicating-programs-paper.md) co-author) — the learnable-self-organization wing of the ALife branch. (2025-06)
- [What Can We Learn About Engineering and Innovation from Half a Century of the Game of Life? (Wolfram, 2025)](sources/wolfram-2025-game-of-life-engineering.md) — [Stephen Wolfram](entities/stephen-wolfram.md) reads 50 years of [Game of Life](entities/game-of-life.md) pattern-discovery as a study of innovation ("metaengineering"): the **construction (invention) vs. search (discovery)** axis, with search overtaking construction as compute grew; the modularity↔comprehensibility trade-off; **computational irreducibility** as a "spark" to be "caged." Opens the wiki's [cellular automata](concepts/alife/cellular-automata.md) concept. (2025-03)
- [Reynolds 1987 — Flocks, Herds, and Schools (SIGGRAPH '87)](sources/reynolds-flocks-herds-schools-1987.md) — **the seminal [boids](concepts/alife/flocking-and-boids.md) paper**; original rule names (Collision Avoidance / Velocity Matching / Flock Centering) in strict precedence, prioritized-acceleration arbitration, localized-perception finding, particle-system/actor lineage, geometric flight, O(N²)+constant-time hypothesis. (1987-07)
- [Boids (Craig Reynolds) — red3d.com](sources/reynolds-boids-page.md) — Reynolds' later overview page for the [boids](concepts/alife/flocking-and-boids.md) model; three rules (separation/alignment/cohesion), *Batman Returns* (1992), "edge of chaos," O(n²)→~O(n). (1986; page maintained)
- [Boids — Stanford SoCo (Wong, 2008)](sources/stanford-soco-boids.md) — student write-up restating the three rules; frames boids within **swarm intelligence**, ant colony optimization, and swarm robotics. (2008-09)
- [Recent Developments and Applications of Drone Swarm (Raj & Kos, 2026)](sources/raj-kos-drone-swarm-review-2026.md) — *Sensors* 26(10):2943 review of UAV swarms; command-and-control taxonomy (consensus/centralized/emergent/hierarchical), trajectory generation + DL prediction, **anti-drone/counter-swarm** (military), swarm metaheuristics (PSO/ACO/…), 1000-drone @ 99.95% from 5% informed agents, LEVIOSA/SwarmGPT LLM interfaces. Anchors [swarm intelligence](concepts/robotics/swarm-intelligence.md). (2026-05-08)
- [Cosmos 3: Omnimodal World Models for Physical AI (Technical Report)](sources/cosmos-3-technical-report.md) — NVIDIA's 138-pg report (led by [Ming-Yu Liu](entities/ming-yu-liu.md)). One **Mixture-of-Transformers** model unifying language/image/video/audio/action for understanding + generation — a [world-action model](concepts/world-models/world-action-model.md) that's also a VLM, video generator, and FD/ID model. Nano (16B) + Super (64B); **#1 open-weight T2I + I2V** (Artificial Analysis) and **#1 RoboArena** policy; beats π0.5 on RoboLab. **Revised edition 2026-06-22** adds a third policy benchmark, **MolmoSpaces** (#1, 39.0% oracle, no benchmark-specific tuning) — a revision a 2026-07-21 hand diff wrongly cleared as "no substantive change"; see the page's Edition history. OpenMDW-1.1. (2026-06-01, rev. 2026-06-22)
- [Develop Physical AI with NVIDIA Cosmos 3 (HF blog)](sources/nvidia-cosmos-3-hf-blog.md) — launch/onboarding companion to the technical report; Nano/Super sizes, Diffusers `Cosmos3OmniPipeline`, SDG datasets, Cosmos-Framework post-training. Omits audio (the report includes it). (2026-06-01)
- [MolmoAct2: Action Reasoning Models for Real-World Deployment (Fang, Duan et al. 2026)](sources/molmoact2-paper.md) — [Ai2](entities/ai2.md)'s **fully-open, deployable** VLA; successor to [MolmoAct](entities/molmoact.md) along 5 axes: [Molmo2-ER](entities/molmo2-er.md) backbone (beats GPT-5/Gemini-ER on 13 embodied benchmarks), three released robot datasets (incl. the 720-hr **largest open bimanual dataset** on [YAM](entities/yam.md)), an open-data [FAST](entities/fast-action-tokenization.md) tokenizer, a hybrid discrete+continuous head via **[per-layer KV conditioning](concepts/learning/per-layer-kv-conditioning.md)**, and **[adaptive-depth reasoning](concepts/learning/adaptive-depth-reasoning.md)** (MolmoAct2-Think). **LIBERO 97.2 / Think 98.1 — top *tier*, [statistically tied](syntheses/platforms/vla-success-rate-audit.md) with OpenVLA-OFT / GR00T N1.7 / π0.5**; runs on a <$6,000 setup at 55.8 Hz. (2026-05-08)
- [Ai2 homepage (allenai.org)](sources/ai2-homepage.md) — how [Ai2](entities/ai2.md) positions itself mid-2026: tagline **"Truly open breakthrough AI"**, organized into **three mission pillars** — AI for Science ([Asta](entities/asta.md) agentic-discovery ecosystem + Semantic Scholar), AI for the Planet ([OlmoEarth](entities/olmoearth.md) earth-observation model + Skylight + EarthRanger), and Embodied AI (the [MolmoAct2](entities/molmoact2.md) line). Reframes Ai2 from "open-LLM lab" to a three-mission org. (continuously updated)
- [GR00T N1 Paper](sources/groot-n1-paper.md) — NVIDIA GEAR's open dual-system humanoid [VLA](concepts/learning/vla-models.md) ([Jim Fan](entities/jim-fan.md) + [Yuke Zhu](entities/yuke-zhu.md); arXiv 2503.14734). Eagle-2 VLM @ 10 Hz + flow-matching DiT @ 120 Hz (2.2B params); the **data pyramid** (8,375.7 h = real robot + human video + DexMimicGen sim + 827 h video-model neural trajectories, ~10× over 88 h of GR-1 teleop); real [Fourier GR-1](entities/fourier-gr-1.md) 76.8% vs Diffusion Policy 46.4%; 10%-data GR00T within 3.8 pts of full-data DP; documented catastrophic-forgetting case. The primary source the 16-source GR00T entity had been missing. (2025-03)
- [The State of Robot Motion Generation (Bekris et al. 2024)](sources/state-of-robot-motion-generation-2024.md) — Rutgers; 50-year cross-community review organized around **explicit-model vs implicit-model** motion generation. First structured wiki coverage of the classical stack ([motion planning](concepts/robotics/motion-planning.md): PRM/RRT*/CHOMP/GCS; [TAMP](concepts/robotics/task-and-motion-planning.md); belief-space planning; control/MPC) alongside the learned stack (LfD/diffusion, RL, VLAs). Verdict: **integration, not supersession**; hedges the internet-scale-data bet the wiki's scaling-law sources make. (2024-12)
- [VQ-BeT Paper](sources/vq-bet-paper.md) — Lee, Wang, Etukuru, Kim, Shafiullah, Pinto (ICML 2024). **Deepened 2026-07-04 from full PDF**: Residual VQ (N_q=2, 8–16 codes/layer) + MinGPT (6/6/120) + focal-loss codes + L1 offset head; SOTA 5/7 unconditional + 6/7 conditional benchmarks; **5× faster inference than Diffusion Policy in sim, 25× on a real Stretch** (where receding-horizon DP fails 0/30); mostly no action chunking. (2024-03)
- [TD-MPC Paper](sources/td-mpc-paper.md) — Hansen, Wang, Su (UCSD; ICML 2022, arXiv 2203.04955). The original [TD-MPC](entities/td-mpc.md): MPPI planning over a decoder-free **TOLD** latent model + TD-learned terminal value; latent-consistency-onto-EMA-teacher (a JEPA-adjacent self-predictive representation, 2022); first documented solve of DMControl Dog (A∈R³⁸); 16× faster wall-clock than LOOP. Fills the TD-MPC1 gap behind the existing TD-MPC2 page. (2022-03)
- [GR00T N1.5 research page](sources/groot-n1_5.md) — NVIDIA GEAR; incremental [GR00T](entities/nvidia-groot.md) update with outsized gains. **Frozen** Eagle 2.5 VLM + simplified adapter + **FLARE** loss + DreamGen neural trajectories. Real GR-1 language-following 46.6%→**93.3%**, success 43.3%→**83.0%**; [Unitree G1](entities/unitree-g1.md) seen-objects 44%→**98.8%**. `nvidia/GR00T-N1.5-3B`. (2025-06-11)
- [GR00T N1.6 research page](sources/groot-n1_6.md) — NVIDIA GEAR; **[Cosmos](entities/nvidia-cosmos.md)-2B reasoning backbone** (first reasoning-integrated GR00T), DiT 16→32 layers, top-4 VLM layers unfrozen, **state-relative action chunks**; adds YAM / AGIBot Genie1 / Galaxea R1 Pro / Unitree G1 whole-body loco-manip data. "Outperforms N1.5" (no published numbers). `nvidia/GR00T-N1.6-3B`. (2025-12-15)
- [Isaac-GR00T GitHub](sources/isaac-gr00t-github.md) — the official [GR00T](entities/nvidia-groot.md) codebase (Apache-2.0 code / NVIDIA Open Model License weights; ~7.5k★). Current: **N1.7 GA** (README dates GA 2026-04-18; `nvidia/GR00T-N1.7-3B`; Cosmos-Reason2-2B / Qwen3-VL backbone, 20K-hr EgoScale pretrain, **state/action dims 29→132, action horizon 16→40**); N1.5/N1.6 on release branches (N1.5 unsupported). LeRobot-v2 + `modality.json`; embodiment tags incl. `NEW_EMBODIMENT` + `UNITREE_G1_SONIC` whole-body; gated Cosmos backbone (HF auth); FFmpeg 4–7 only; fine-tune 40 GB+, inference 16 GB+; Thor/Spark now both CUDA 13 + Py 3.12. (2025-03; re-ingested 2026-07-07)
- [DreamGen Paper](sources/dreamgen-paper.md) — [NVIDIA GEAR](entities/nvidia-gear.md); turns image-to-video models into **synthetic robot-data generators** ("neural trajectories"). 4-stage pipeline (fine-tune WAN2.1 → rollout → IDM/LAPA pseudo-actions → policy training); behavior gen 11.2%→43.2%, environment gen 0%→28.5%; DreamGen Bench correlates with downstream policy success. The root of the Dream\* line + the neural-trajectory layer of the [GR00T](entities/nvidia-groot.md) data pyramid. (2025-06)
- [FLARE Paper](sources/flare-paper.md) — [NVIDIA GEAR](entities/nvidia-gear.md); **Future LAtent REpresentation Alignment** — a JEPA-adjacent auxiliary loss adding implicit world modeling to a flow-matching policy (align learnable future tokens to a future-observation embedding, λ=0.2). Up to +26%; human-video co-training doubles novel-object success. **The auxiliary loss [GR00T N1.5](sources/groot-n1_5.md) adopts.** (2025-05)
- [Eagle Paper (mixture of encoders)](sources/eagle-paper.md) — NVIDIA (ICLR 2025); the original [Eagle](entities/eagle-vlm.md) VLM study — **channel-concatenation of complementary vision encoders** beats complex fusion; pre-alignment stage; strong OCR/high-res. Research foundation of the Eagle-2 model that backs [GR00T N1](sources/groot-n1-paper.md). (2024-08)
- [Eagle 2.5 Paper (long-context)](sources/eagle-2-5-paper.md) — NVIDIA; frontier long-context VLM; **drops mixture-of-encoders for a single SigLIP-so400M** + information-first sampling + 32K→128K progressive training; Eagle2.5-8B hits 72.4% Video-MME at 8B. The VLM backbone of [GR00T N1.5](sources/groot-n1_5.md). (2025-04)
- [Bringing AI Closer to the Edge and On-Device with Gemma 4 (NVIDIA blog)](sources/nvidia-gemma-4-edge-blog.md) — [Gemma 4](entities/gemma4.md) across NVIDIA hardware (Jetson Orin Nano E2B/E4B → DGX Spark 31B BF16). First MoE Gemma; 4 variants; NVFP4; vLLM/Ollama/llama.cpp/NIM. Explicit **physical-AI / on-device robotics** framing. The "what runs where" reference for the [on-device agents synthesis](syntheses/agents/on-device-and-on-robot-agents.md). (2026-04-02)
- [NemoClaw Quickstart with Hermes (NVIDIA docs)](sources/nvidia-nemoclaw-hermes-quickstart.md) — concrete recipe to run [Hermes](entities/hermes-agent.md) as a NemoClaw agent variant (`nemohermes` + OpenShell sandbox; dashboard 18789 / OpenAI API 8642; default `nemotron-3-super-120b-a12b` via NVIDIA endpoints; network-policy tiers). Resolves several [NemoClaw](entities/nemoclaw.md)/Hermes open questions. (2026)
- [SONIC Paper](sources/sonic-paper.md) — [NVIDIA GEAR](entities/nvidia-gear.md); **[GEAR-SONIC](entities/gear-sonic.md)** humanoid whole-body controller (the `UNITREE_G1_SONIC` controller). Motion-tracking-as-scaling-task PPO policy (42M params / 611 h mocap); 99.6% success, 41% MPJPE cut vs BeyondMimic, 99.2% sim-to-real on [Unitree G1](entities/unitree-g1.md); FSQ universal-token interface a [GR00T N1.5](sources/groot-n1_5.md) VLA drives for loco-manipulation (5-task avg 75%). arXiv 2511.07820. (2025-11)
- [BumbleBee — From Experts to a Generalist (WBC)](sources/bumblebee-experts-to-generalist-wbc.md) — [BeingBeyond](entities/beingbeyond.md) + Peking Univ ([Zongqing Lu](entities/zongqing-lu.md)); **expert→generalist humanoid [whole-body control](concepts/robotics/whole-body-control.md)**. Cluster AMASS motions (semantic+kinematic AE) → per-cluster RL experts → iterative delta-action sim-to-real → DAgger-distill into one Gated-Transformer-XL generalist. **SOTA on [Unitree G1](entities/unitree-g1.md)** — MuJoCo SR 66.84% vs Exbody2 50.19% (all others <40%); semantic clustering is the ablation that matters. Modest compute (2× RTX 4090). arXiv 2506.12779. (2025-09)
- [MotionBricks Paper](sources/motionbricks-paper.md) — [NVIDIA](entities/nvidia.md) ([Xue Bin Peng](entities/xue-bin-peng.md), [Yuke Zhu](entities/yuke-zhu.md) senior); **real-time modular latent motion model** spanning **animation + robotics**. 350k-clip single-model backbone + **"smart primitives"** authoring interface (zero-shot, no fine-tuning); **15,000 FPS / 2 ms latency**; UE5 production demo + [Unitree G1](entities/unitree-g1.md) [WBC](concepts/robotics/whole-body-control.md) deploy. Sibling to [SONIC](sources/sonic-paper.md) (adds the object-interaction axis SONIC lacks). SIGGRAPH 2026; CC-BY 4.0; arXiv 2604.24833. (2026-04)
- [GR00T-WholeBodyControl GitHub](sources/gr00t-wholebodycontrol-github.md) — NVlabs; the **unified code platform** for [GEAR-SONIC](entities/gear-sonic.md) + [MotionBricks](sources/motionbricks-paper.md) + **Decoupled WBC** (the [GR00T](entities/nvidia-groot.md) N1.5/N1.6 controllers, named here for the first time) + a PICO VR-teleop stack. Train (`gear_sonic`, PPO/Isaac Lab) → deploy (`gear_sonic_deploy`, C++/TensorRT) on [Unitree G1](entities/unitree-g1.md). 2.9k★; Apache-2.0 code + NVIDIA Open Model License weights. (2026)
- [WBC-AGILE GitHub](sources/wbc-agile-github.md) — nvidia-isaac; **AGILE** = "A Generic Isaac-Lab based Engine for humanoid loco-manipulation learning" (NVIDIA + ETH). Reusable Isaac-Lab RL engine (teacher-student distillation), validated sim-to-real on **[Unitree G1](entities/unitree-g1.md) + [Booster T1](entities/booster-t1.md)**; the "AGILE" under [Isaac Teleop](entities/nvidia-isaac-teleop.md) in the GR00T e2e workflow. 295★; v1.2. (2026-03-24)
- [GR00T end-to-end workflow docs](sources/nvidia-gr00t-e2e-workflow-docs.md) — NVIDIA Physical-AI course: teleop→collect→fine-tune→eval→deploy for [GR00T 1.7](entities/nvidia-groot.md) on [Unitree G1](entities/unitree-g1.md), two paths (Isaac Lab-Arena sim / [Jetson Thor](entities/jetson-thor.md) real). Stack: [Isaac Teleop](entities/nvidia-isaac-teleop.md)+AGILE, [LeRobot](entities/lerobot.md), [Isaac ROS](entities/isaac-ros.md); task = tabletop pick-and-place. The first-party template behind the [pinball-robot project](syntheses/projects/pinball-playing-robot.md). (2026)
- [Robot Security Framework (RSF) GitHub](sources/aliasrobotics-rsf-github.md) — [Alias Robotics](entities/alias-robotics.md); standardized **robot-cybersecurity** assessment methodology across 4 layers (Physical/Network/Firmware/Application); targets [ROS 2](entities/ros2.md) + MISRA. The classical-infosec complement to the wiki's LLM-agent [input-rail / guardrails](concepts/safety/ai-guardrails.md) thread. GPL-3.0; arXiv 1806.04042. (2018 / rolling)
- [Gemini Robotics 1.5 tech report](sources/gemini-robotics-1-5-report.md) — [Google DeepMind](entities/google-deepmind.md); the wiki's first deep [Gemini Robotics](entities/gemini-robotics.md) source. GR 1.5 VLA (one checkpoint across [ALOHA](entities/aloha.md)/[Franka](entities/franka-panda.md)/[Apollo](entities/apptronik-apollo.md)) + GR-ER 1.5 reasoning orchestrator on Gemini 2.5; **Motion Transfer** (zero-shot cross-embodiment), **Embodied Thinking** (0.26→0.55 multi-step), agentic orchestration halves failures (22% vs 44.5%); ASIMOV-2.0 safety. (2025)
- [i2RT YAM Arm Documentation](sources/i2rt-yam-docs.md) — [YAM](entities/yam.md) ("Yet Another Manipulator"); 6-DOF CAN-bus tabletop arm for embodied-AI data collection; $2,999–$4,999; MuJoCo sim + Python SDK; a bimanual YAM pair is a [GR00T N1.6](sources/groot-n1_6.md) teleop-data source. (2026)
- [Galaxea R1 User Guide](sources/galaxea-r1-user-guide.md) — [Galaxea R1](entities/galaxea-r1.md); wheeled dual-arm humanoid (24 DOF; 0–200 cm; 5 kg; 550 TOPS); simulated R1 Pro on BEHAVIOR is a [GR00T N1.6](sources/groot-n1_6.md) data embodiment. (2026)
- [Standard Bots — AI page](sources/standardbots-ai-page.md) — [Standard Bots](entities/standard-bots.md); *"first self-serve AI platform for industrial robots"* — commercial learn-by-demonstration for 6-axis arms (RO1/Core 18 kg $37k); teleop→cloud-train→autonomous; the industrial counterpoint to the research-VLA line. (2026)
- [Awesome-Embodied-Robotics-and-Agent (GitHub list)](sources/awesome-embodied-robotics-agent.md) — zchoi's curated index (~1.8k★, ~200+ entries) of embodied-AI + LLM/VLM work; a coverage cross-check flagging wiki gaps (vision-language navigation, household simulators). (2026)
- [BEHAVIOR-1K Paper](sources/behavior-1k-paper.md) — Stanford (Li, Zhang, …, Martín-Martín; CoRL 2022, arXiv 2403.09227). The [BEHAVIOR-1K](entities/behavior-benchmark.md) benchmark: **1,000 survey-grounded household activities / 50 scenes / 9,000+ objects**, defined in **BDDL**, simulated in **[OmniGibson](entities/omnigibson.md)** (Omniverse + PhysX 5; deformables + fluids + extended states). The hard end of the [sim-to-real gap](concepts/learning/sim-to-real-transfer.md): end-to-end RL **0.0**, real-robot **0–22%**. (2022; arXiv 2024-03)
- [OmniGibson GitHub (BIT-PIE fork) codebase](sources/omnigibson-github.md) — dedicated codebase ingest of [OmniGibson](entities/omnigibson.md) via the user's [`BIT-PIE/OmniGibson`](https://github.com/BIT-PIE/OmniGibson) fork (MIT, v1.1.0). Needs **[Isaac Sim](entities/nvidia-isaac-sim.md) 4.1.0** (`isaacsim-for-omnigibson`); OpenAI Gym; **14 robots** across mobile/manipulation/mobile-manip/VR (incl. [Galaxea R1/R1Pro](entities/galaxea-r1.md), [Stretch](entities/stretch.md), [Tiago](entities/tiago.md), [Franka](entities/franka-panda.md), VX300S); ships 1,004 BEHAVIOR-1K tasks + 50 scenes. Confirms R1Pro-on-BEHAVIOR = the GR00T N1.6 sim substrate. (2024-10 fork)

- [Seeed Studio Jetson Product-Line Selection Guide](sources/seeed-jetson-selection-guide.md) — Jetson module ladder (Orin Nano 67 / Orin NX 157 / AGX Orin 275 TOPS / Thor 2070 FP4-TFLOPS) + Seeed's **reComputer** carrier mapping (J30xx → Orin Nano, J40xx → Orin NX, **J4012 = Orin NX 16 GB**). full article + infographic (user-supplied; blog 403). Series→module→use-case mapping + by-scenario decision tree; the buy-it-as-a-carrier bridge for the [onboard-compute comparison](syntheses/platforms/jetson-onboard-compute-xlerobot.md). Confirms a **battery-powered Robotics J30/40 line** (19–54 V, CAN+GMSL, 157 TOPS @ 60 °C/40 W) — the XLeRobot Orin NX carrier — up through the Thor **J601** humanoid "brain+cerebellum" (EtherCAT). Prices not in the article. (2026-05-13)

- [How to Choose the Right NVIDIA Jetson Carrier Board (Seeed, Liyan Gong)](sources/seeed-jetson-carrier-board-selection.md) — carrier-board **selection methodology**: confirm module tier → carrier is part of system design → make priorities explicit (size/connectivity/expandability can't all be optimized). Example carriers (Super J401 / Robotics J401 / J401 / A603 / A608 / Mini J501) mapped to priorities via a worked edge-AI-vision case. Companion to the [selection guide](sources/seeed-jetson-selection-guide.md) (J401 carrier ↔ J40xx product). (2026-02-09)

- [Raspberry Pi AI HAT+ 2 (Hailo-10H)](sources/raspberry-pi-ai-hat-plus-2.md) — official product page; **Hailo-10H** generative-AI HAT for [Pi 5](entities/raspberry-pi-5.md): 40 TOPS INT4, 8 GB on-board RAM, $180; runs local **LLMs/VLMs** ("a HAT that speaks your language"), vision parity with the 26-TOPS AI HAT+. The wiki's first **NPU (non-CUDA)** onboard option for an [XLeRobot](entities/xlerobot.md) Pi build — good for an onboard agent/LLM layer, but does **not** run LeRobot control policies (see [Hailo](entities/hailo.md)). (2026)
- [hailo-apps (GitHub)](sources/hailo-apps-github.md) — Hailo's MIT app repo; 30+ ready-to-run apps for Hailo-8/8L/10H on Pi 5 / Ubuntu / Windows. Vision CLIs (`hailo-detect`/`-pose`/`-seg`/`-depth`, YOLO26) + Hailo-10H-only `gen_ai_apps` (LLM/VLM/speech, Voice2Action) + an "AI-Powered Development" agent beta. The software that runs on the [AI HAT+ 2](sources/raspberry-pi-ai-hat-plus-2.md). (rel. 26.03.1, 2026-04-13)
- [ZeroMQ — official website (zeromq.org)](sources/zeromq-org.md) — homepage of the brokerless messaging library: sockets carrying atomic messages over inproc/IPC/TCP/UDP/TIPC/multicast/WebSocket; pub-sub / push-pull / request-reply patterns; async I/O core; Guide with 750 examples in 28 languages; adopters incl. Jupyter (kernel protocol) + Bitcoin + Spotify. Ingested as the transport under [Isaac-GR00T](sources/isaac-gr00t-github.md)'s ZMQ policy server/client. (rolling; snapshot 2026-07-07)
- [NVIDIA Isaac Teleop and GR00T 1.7 in LeRobot (HF blog)](sources/nvidia-isaac-teleop-gr00t17-lerobot-blog.md) — NVIDIA (7 authors). **[GR00T](entities/nvidia-groot.md) 1.7 GA as a native [LeRobot](entities/lerobot.md) policy** (`policy.type=groot`, identical weights to the open-source path; **N1.5 no longer supported**; Dataset v3.0 required) + **[Isaac Teleop](entities/nvidia-isaac-teleop.md)** launch (leader-arm or XR-headset demo collection; `isaacteleop` + CloudXR). First published N1.7 numbers: **[LIBERO](entities/libero.md) avg 96.5% vs 87%** (GR00T 1.5; vendor self-comparison), per-suite checkpoints released. Full walkthrough on the $100-class [SO-101](entities/so-arm101.md); `lerobot-rollout` + RTC inference; [DGX Spark](entities/dgx-spark.md) CUDA-13 torch pin. (2026-07-07)
- [Isaac GR00T docs — TensorRT optimization](sources/isaac-gr00t-tensorrt-deployment-docs.md) — official deployment docs; **first NVIDIA-published GR00T latency on Jetson** (N1.6-3B, batch 1, end-to-end): **[Thor](entities/jetson-thor.md) 117→92 ms (10.9 Hz)**, **AGX Orin 300→173 ms (5.8 Hz)**, RTX 5090 31 ms (32.1 Hz). Only the DiT action head is TRT-compiled (VLM stays eager); BF16 recommended; engines per-GPU-arch; Thor's 1.27× speedup is the table's weakest (no NVFP4 path yet). Orin NX/Nano absent. (rolling; snapshot 2026-07-08)
- [NVIDIA forums — real-time VLA inference on Thor & RTX (π0.5 / GR00T N1.6–1.7)](sources/nvidia-forum-thor-realtime-vla-inference.md) — community developer's hand-written-CUDA-kernel results: **GR00T N1.6 41–45 ms (22–24 Hz) and π0.5 44 ms (23 Hz) on [Thor](entities/jetson-thor.md)**; RTX 5090 76–80 Hz — ~2× the official TensorRT path. Single reporter, unreplicated; existence proof of Thor headroom. (2026-05-02)
- [NVIDIA and Hugging Face Bring New Models and Frameworks to LeRobot (NVIDIA blog)](sources/nvidia-hf-lerobot-open-robotics-blog.md) — corporate umbrella of the July 2026 NVIDIA↔[HF](entities/hugging-face.md) partnership ("3M robotics devs × 16M AI builders"): [GR00T](entities/nvidia-groot.md) 1.7 + [Isaac Teleop](entities/nvidia-isaac-teleop.md) (detailed in the [HF blog](sources/nvidia-isaac-teleop-gr00t17-lerobot-blog.md)) **plus three new items** — **[Cosmos 3](entities/nvidia-cosmos.md) → [LeRobot](entities/lerobot.md) "soon"**, **Isaac Lab-Arena ↔ LeRobot EnvHub** (first EnvHub sighting), **[Thor](entities/jetson-thor.md) + [Reachy 2](entities/reachy.md)** open-humanoid VLA deployment; claims "largest open physical-AI dataset" (15M+ downloads, 350k+ trajectories, 57M grasps, unnamed). (2026-07-06)
- [Jetson Thor T3000/T2000 blog](sources/nvidia-jetson-thor-t3000-t2000-blog.md) — NVIDIA expands the [Jetson Thor](entities/jetson-thor.md) family downward: **T3000 (32 GB, 865 TFLOPS), T2000 (16 GB, 400 TFLOPS), IGX T3000** (functional safety + NVIDIA Halos for Robotics). Line now spans "70 TOPS–2,000 TFLOPS"; **Cosmos 3 Edge (4B)** to Thor; Jetson Agent Skills (auto memory-opt). GA **Q1 2027**. Breaks the "Thor = 128 GB" assumption ([Thor-vs-Spark](syntheses/platforms/jetson-thor-vs-dgx-spark.md)) for the lower tiers; the low-power tiers speak to the [XLeRobot power budget](syntheses/projects/xlerobot-thor-power-budget.md). (2026-07-15)
- [NVIDIA Halos for Robotics](sources/nvidia-halos-robotics.md) — NVIDIA's **full-stack functional-safety system** ([NVIDIA Halos](entities/nvidia-halos.md)) for physical AI: IGX [Thor](entities/jetson-thor.md) + Functional Safety Island, **Halos OS** (Linux+QNX), Holoscan SensorBridge; **Inside-Out** (onboard, [Digit](entities/digit.md)) vs **Outside-In** (external cameras / virtual zones, TÜV forklift). First **ANAB-accredited** AI-functional-safety inspection program; "AV-Proven, Robotics-Ready." The productized "certified safety layer under a learned policy" the [safety-standards](concepts/robotics/robot-safety-standards.md) page predicted. (2026-07-15)
- [Halos Outside-In Safety Blueprint (GitHub)](sources/halos-outside-in-safety-github.md) — NVIDIA; open-source (Apache-2.0, early access) code for [Halos](entities/nvidia-halos.md)' **Outside-In** side. Fixed infrastructure cameras → **Metropolis VSS perception** → **Safety Core** (OISF/ex-PSF) → MUTE/UNMUTE decision; SIL/HIL via [Isaac Sim](entities/nvidia-isaac-sim.md); reference use case = automated trailer loading. Ships a **Claude Code deploy skill**. Not production-certified on its own. 54★. (2026-07-15)
- [Walden Robotics — Launch from Stealth](sources/walden-robotics-launch.md) — **[Russ Tedrake](entities/russ-tedrake.md)'s startup revealed** (the wiki's tracked "stealth" venture). **[Walden Robotics](entities/walden-robotics.md)**: spun out of [TRI](entities/tri.md) Jan 2026, launched from stealth; **$300M seed / $1.1B**, Toyota-co-led. General-purpose **manufacturing/logistics** robots on **[LBMs](concepts/learning/large-behavior-models.md) + [Diffusion Policy](entities/diffusion-policy.md)** with autonomy + human-remote-assist; in production at a Toyota NC plant since Feb 2026. Team = TRI robot-learning leadership ([Burchfiel](entities/ben-burchfiel.md) CTO, [Feng](entities/siyuan-feng.md) Principal Architect, Gaidon CSO, Ambrus Head of AI). Name references **Thoreau**, not LBMs. (2026-07-15)
- [Automated Podcast — MIT's Russ Tedrake Says Robotics Is Finally on a Rocket Ship](sources/automated-podcast-tedrake-rocket-ship.md) — 47-min Brian Heater (A3) interview at Mass Robotics; ingested via auto-captions. **[Tedrake](entities/russ-tedrake.md) confirms a stealth physical AI startup** (now revealed as **[Walden Robotics](entities/walden-robotics.md)**, 2026-07-15); the definitional **[LBM](concepts/learning/large-behavior-models.md)-vs-VLA taxonomy** (LBM = images→actions superclass; VLA = uptrained-VLM subtype; video backbone for long context); the **"build a bridge" reframe** of robot-data scarcity (start from a base model's common sense, add one output); multitask pre-training robustness; deployment = next milestone; "amplify, not replace" + labor-economist conversations; Drake = "my horcrux." (2026-07-01)
- [TRI LBM paper — A Careful Examination of Large Behavior Models for Multitask Dexterous Manipulation](sources/tri-lbm-paper.md) — **the [LBM](concepts/learning/large-behavior-models.md) program's primary source** (82 TRI authors, [Tedrake](entities/russ-tedrake.md) senior; arXiv 2025-07 → Science Robotics 2026). Diffusion transformer (ViT VL encoders + AdaLN head, 16-step/1.6 s chunks — **not a VLA**) pretrained on ~1,695 h (bimanual Franka teleop + sim + [UMI](entities/umi.md) + [OXE](entities/open-x-embodiment.md)); **blind randomized A/B eval** (1,800 real + 47k sim rollouts, Clopper-Pearson CIs): multitask pretraining → **3–5× less fine-tune data**, smooth predictable scaling, weak zero-shot; **50 rollouts → 20–30-pt CI width** (most robot-learning evals are underpowered); normalization choices often dominate architecture. Sim = Drake. (2025-07-07)
- [History-Guided Video Diffusion (DFoT paper)](sources/history-guided-video-diffusion-paper.md) — Song & Chen*, Simchowitz, Du, [Tedrake](entities/russ-tedrake.md), Sitzmann (MIT/CMU/Harvard, ICML 2025; raw PDF). **Diffusion Forcing Transformer**: per-frame independent noise levels in a non-causal DiT ⇒ condition on *any* history subset; **History Guidance** (vanilla/temporal/fractional score composition) ⇒ Kinetics-600 **FVD 4.3** (matches MAGVIT-v2 at ~1/10 industry compute), OOD-history robustness, **862-frame rollout from one image**, and a physical-robot IL result (**83%**, memory+reactivity composed at sampling time from data that never contained both). VDMs fine-tune into DFoT at ~12.5% cost. (2025-02-10; v2 2025-07-24)
- [awesome-physical-ai (GitHub curated list)](sources/awesome-physical-ai-github.md) — solo-curated (natnew, 111★, MIT) full-stack Physical-AI directory (simulators → datasets → benchmarks → models → safety/governance → production → courses/companies/hardware/people). Ingested as a **gap-mining/second-opinion catalog**: surfaces whole categories the wiki lacks — robot-safety standards (ISO 13482/10218, CBFs), MBRL world models (Dreamer/TD-MPC2/Genie 2), locomotion corpus (RMA/legged_gym/H2O line), evaluation methodology (rliable/robomimic/RoboArena). Curation caveats noted (solo list; at least one mislabeled entry). (rolling; snapshot 2026-07-08)
- [Fosch-Villaronga et al. — ISO 13482 and robotic exoskeletons (Tech. in Society 2023)](sources/fosch-villaronga-iso13482-exoskeletons.md) — **primary academic critique of [ISO 13482](concepts/robotics/robot-safety-standards.md)** (Leiden eLaw + CSIC; CC-BY; raw PDF). Systematic review (71 works) → **six gap areas** w/ policy recommendations: missing hazards (cognitive/psychological, overtrust, third parties), insufficient requirements, **no V&V methods** (no defined way to demonstrate conformity), info-for-use, normative refs, language; plus "personal care" undefined, medical-device boundary unclear, categories too heterogeneous (→ per-category standards), physical-safety-only focus. ERC Safe & Sound program. (2023-10-13)
- [S5WM — Accelerating MBRL with State-Space World Models](sources/s5wm-paper.md) — Krinner*/Aljalbout*/Romero/Scaramuzza (UZH RPG; arXiv 2025-02; raw PDF). Swaps [Dreamer](entities/dreamer.md)'s sequential RSSM for a **parallelizable S5 SSM** (episode-boundary resets) → **10× faster WM training / 4× overall MBRL wall-clock** at equal sample efficiency; asymmetric **privileged-state reconstruction** for vision-POMDP sim-to-real; validated on **real agile-quadrotor / drone-racing** hardware. (2025-02-27)
- [EAWM — Event-Aware World Model for RL](sources/eawm-paper.md) — Peng et al. (Tsinghua/Zhejiang, **ICLR 2026**; raw PDF). Cognitive-science prior: segment observation streams into **events** (automated generator + Generic Event Segmentor) and shape the latent space via event prediction; unified WM formulation instantiated as EADream/EASimulus → **+13% Atari 100K / +10% Craftax / +19% DMC / +45% DMC-GB2**, SOTA across 55 tasks, fixed hyperparameters; code released. (2026-01)
- [World Models (Ha & Schmidhuber, 2018)](sources/world-models-paper.md) — **the founding paper of the genre** (raw PDF; NeurIPS 2018). V-M-C: [VAE](concepts/learning/variational-autoencoder.md) + MDN-RNN + 867-param CMA-ES controller; solved CarRacing-v0 from pixels; **trained a policy entirely inside the model's "hallucinated dream"** (VizDoom) with temperature τ against model exploitation. Closes the wiki's dangling "Ha & Schmidhuber 2018" citations. (2018-03-27)
- [PlaNet — Learning Latent Dynamics for Planning from Pixels](sources/planet-paper.md) — [Hafner](entities/danijar-hafner.md) et al., ICML 2019 (raw PDF). Origin of the **RSSM** + latent overshooting; CEM planning in latent space, no policy network; ~**200× less interaction** than A3C on DMC. The backbone [S5WM](sources/s5wm-paper.md) later replaces. (2018-11)
- [MuZero (Nature 2020)](sources/muzero-paper.md) — Schrittwieser et al., DeepMind (raw PDF). Tree search with a **learned value-equivalent model** (predicts only reward/policy/value — no reconstruction, no rules): AlphaZero-level Go/chess/shogi **without the rules**, SOTA Atari-57. The decision-time-planning pole of MBRL, opposite [Dreamer](entities/dreamer.md)'s imagination pole. (2019-11)
- [EfficientZero — Mastering Atari with Limited Data](sources/efficientzero-paper.md) — Ye et al., NeurIPS 2021 (raw PDF). [MuZero](sources/muzero-paper.md) + self-supervised consistency + value-prefix + off-policy correction → **194.3% mean HNS on Atari 100K in 2 h of gameplay** — first super-human at that budget, ~500× less data than DQN. Context for every 100K number ([EAWM](sources/eawm-paper.md), [DIAMOND](sources/diamond-paper.md)). (2021-11)
- [DayDreamer — World Models for Physical Robot Learning](sources/daydreamer-paper.md) — Wu*/Escontrela*/[Hafner](entities/danijar-hafner.md)*, Goldberg, Abbeel; CoRL 2022 (raw PDF). **[Dreamer](entities/dreamer.md) on 4 real robots, online, no simulator**: A1 quadruped from lying-down to walking **in 1 hour without resets** (+10-min perturbation adaptation); UR5/XArm visual pick-place; Sphero nav. The real-robot existence proof for imagination-based MBRL — and the explicit alternative to sim-to-real. (2022-06)
- [DIAMOND — Diffusion for World Modeling](sources/diamond-paper.md) — Alonso*/Jelley* et al., NeurIPS 2024 (raw PDF). **Diffusion model as the world model** (discrete latents discard decision-relevant pixels): **1.46 mean HNS on Atari 100K**, best for agents trained entirely in imagination; 87 h of CS:GO → playable **neural game engine** (Dust II). The bridge between the wiki's MBRL and video-diffusion threads; ancestor of the Genie/[Cosmos](entities/nvidia-cosmos.md) playable-world wave. (2024-05)
- [Aging in Place: Growing Older at Home (NIA)](sources/nia-aging-in-place.md) — NIH consumer guide; the demand-side needs taxonomy (ADLs/IADLs) behind assistive robotics; names no robots, only wearable emergency-alert systems. (2023-10-12)
- [The Waymo World Model blog](sources/waymo-world-model.md) — generative-video world model for autonomous-driving simulation; built on Google DeepMind's [Genie 3](entities/genie-3.md); camera+lidar multi-sensor output. (2026-02-06)
- [Sensori Robotics — Yuri (company site)](sources/sensori-robotics-yuri.md) — integrated dual-arm (2× 7-DOF) Physical-AI rig, Desktop + Mobile; bilateral force-feedback teleop; LeRobot recording; open OpenArm+/OpenBase; quote-only price. (ingested 2026-07-13)
- [NeMo Guardrails — Library Overview](sources/nemo-guardrails-library-overview.md) — primary-source docs for the guardrail layer: **five rails** (input / retrieval / dialog / **execution** / output), Colang + YAML config, and the full guardrails library (NemoGuard NIMs, LlamaGuard, Presidio/GLiNER PII, ActiveFence, Cisco AI Defense, …). Two facts that matter for robots: **execution rails validate tool calls** (but ship *empty* — the policy is yours), and the guardrails server is an **OpenAI-compatible `/v1/chat/completions` proxy** (so text rails are a base-URL swap). No latency benchmarks anywhere. (rolling docs; ingested 2026-07-13)
- [Safeguard Agentic AI Systems with the NVIDIA Safety Recipe](sources/nvidia-safety-recipe-agentic-ai.md) — NVIDIA's **build → deploy → run** safety lifecycle for agentic LLMs: policy-to-eval, safety post-training (content safety 88→94%, product security 56→63%, no accuracy loss), then runtime [guardrails](concepts/safety/ai-guardrails.md) (content safety / topic control / jailbreak detect NIMs) + [garak](entities/garak.md) scanning. **Artifact deprecated 2026-04-22** → NeMo Microservices; the architecture is what survives. The wiki's first primary source on the safety layer that every [LLM-agent robot](concepts/agents/llm-agent-architecture.md) here lacks. (2025-07-17)
- [Agile Robots launches Agile ONE](sources/agile-robots-agile-one-launch.md) — Munich DLR-spinout [Agile Robots](entities/agile-robots.md) enters the humanoid race with **[Agile ONE](entities/agile-one.md)** (174 cm / 69 kg / 20 kg payload / 2 m/s / **21-DOF hands** with per-joint force-torque sensing). Distinct **integration-not-standalone** strategy (humanoid + AMRs + arms + vision under AgileCore); **built in Bavaria**, series production 2026; [Google DeepMind](entities/google-deepmind.md) + Deutsche Telekom AI partners. The wiki's first **European industrial humanoid**. (2025-11-19)
- [Techman Robot — Company Profile](sources/techman-robot-about.md) — Taiwan's [Techman Robot](entities/techman-robot.md) (Quanta subsidiary), first cobots with **built-in vision**; world's **#2 cobot brand** (~10% vs Universal Robots' ~50%, 2021); Omron 10% stake (2021). The wiki's first primary source on **[collaborative robots (cobots)](concepts/robotics/collaborative-robots.md)** — the classical industrial-automation lineage of "AI robotics." (2026)
- [URKL humanoid robot combat league](sources/urkl-robot-combat-league.md) — the **world's first humanoid free-combat league** (Shenzhen), organized by **[EngineAI](entities/engineai.md)**; 200+ teams from 10+ countries fight with identical **[T800](entities/engineai-t800.md)** robots (173 cm / 75 kg / 450 N·m joints / world-first solid-state-battery humanoid) for a **~$1.44M** belt. Framed as a real-world [whole-body-control](concepts/robotics/whole-body-control.md) [testbed](concepts/robotics/robot-combat-sports.md) ("shortens iteration 30%+"). (2026-02-09)
- [Shenzhen Story — URKL combat tournament (YouTube)](sources/shenzhen-story-urkl-youtube.md) — 70-s report from the [URKL](sources/urkl-robot-combat-league.md) finals at Shenzhen's Nanshan Cultural and Sports Center; confirms venue, the 200-teams→top-32 funnel, and [EngineAI T800](entities/engineai-t800.md) as sole platform. "Today they fight in the ring; tomorrow they work alongside us." (2026-07-16)
- [Inside NVIDIA Halos for Robotics (developer blog)](sources/nvidia-halos-robotics-blog.md) — technical deep-dive on [NVIDIA Halos](entities/nvidia-halos.md) as a **3-layer functional-safety system**: IGX Thor safety silicon (IEC 61508 **SIL 3** FSI, **22,000+ safety mechanisms**, 2,070 FP4 TFLOPS), Halos OS (Linux + optional QNX; 18k eng-yrs / 7M LOC AV heritage), the full **Outside-In pipeline** (SIPP→SAIM→SEI→SDM), the **ANAB-accredited Inspection Lab**, and NVIDIA's IEC 61508 **Convenor** standards role. Answers the Halos entity's "which standards?" question. (2026-06-22)
- [RoboArena: Distributed Real-World Evaluation of Generalist Robot Policies](sources/roboarena-paper.md) — Atreya, [Pertsch](entities/karl-pertsch.md), …, [Finn](entities/chelsea-finn.md), [Levine](entities/sergey-levine.md) (32 authors, CoRL 2025). **The real-world pole of evaluation methodology.** Evaluators at 7 institutions freely choose tasks but run **double-blind pairwise A/B** on shared [DROID](entities/droid.md); an extended **Bradley-Terry** model (ability θ / task-difficulty τ / policy×task ψ, EM-fit) yields a ranking. **Pearson r ≈ 0.95 with oracle vs ≈ 0.60 for conventional centralized eval**, converging in **~100 comparisons** — a direct measurement that standard success-rate ranking is bad. 4,284 episodes / 612 comparisons / 7 policies; π₀-FAST-DROID strongest; discrete tokenization > diffusion. Cost: ordering, not magnitude. (2025-06-22)
- [LIBERO-PRO: Beyond Memorization](sources/libero-pro-paper.md) — Zhou, Xu, Tie et al. Perturbs [LIBERO](entities/libero.md) along objects / initial states / instructions / environments. **Models scoring >90% on standard LIBERO collapse to 0.0%** — because *the evaluation tasks are the training tasks*, differing only by imperceptible initial-state variation. Models **keep grasping after the target object is swapped** and emit **unchanged actions under corrupted instructions**. Tested OpenVLA / π0 / π0.5 (π0.5 most robust at 0.38). **Also confirms the protocol: 50 episodes/task → 500/suite, 2,000 per four-suite average**, grounding the [success-rate audit](syntheses/platforms/vla-success-rate-audit.md). (2025-10-04)
- [RoboLab project page](sources/nvidia-robolab-project.md) — **[RoboLab](entities/nvidia-robolab.md)**, NVIDIA SRL's **robot-/policy-agnostic sim benchmark** for real-world task-generalist policies; language-generated scenes/tasks resist saturation; **RoboLab-120** (120 tasks, 68.7% DROID vocab overlap). The benchmark [Cosmos 3](entities/nvidia-cosmos.md) policies are scored on. Closes a dangling in-wiki reference. RSS 2026. (2026)
- [NVIDIA DeepStream SDK (GitHub)](sources/nvidia-deepstream-github.md) — NVIDIA's **[DeepStream](entities/nvidia-deepstream.md)** GStreamer-based streaming-analytics SDK (v9.1, July 2026); multi-stream multi-model inference on Jetson→dGPU; TensorRT + tracking + Triton/TAO/**Metropolis**. The perception plumbing under Metropolis-VSS that [Halos Outside-In](entities/nvidia-halos.md) safety runs on. (2026-07)
- [Jetson Device Skills (GitHub)](sources/jetson-device-skills-github.md) — NVIDIA's catalog of **[agent skills](concepts/agents/agent-skills.md)** ([JetPack](entities/jetpack.md) 7.2) teaching Claude Code / Cursor / Codex to inspect + tune a **live Jetson** ([8 skills](entities/jetson-device-skills.md): diagnostics, memory-audit, LLM-serve/benchmark, speculative-decoding, …); portable `SKILL.md` + helper scripts; Apache-2.0/CC-BY-4.0. (2026)
- [AI Coding on Jetson with Claude Code and NVIDIA Skills (JetsonHacks)](sources/jetsonhacks-ai-coding-jetson-claude-code.md) — ~13-min practitioner demo: VS Code + Claude Code + [Jetson Device Skills](entities/jetson-device-skills.md) + remote SSH (AGX Orin host → headless Orin Nano); JetsonHacks poll = **Claude Code most popular agent 2:1**; thesis "**the agent works for you, not vice-versa**." (2026-07-07)
- [Enhancing YOLOv11n for Reliable Child Detection (PTIT 2026)](sources/ptit-yolov11n-child-detection.md) — architecture-free edge-CV recipe: fine-tuned **YOLOv11n** + scene-aware **synthetic-child compositing / CCTV-degradation augmentation** + **[SAHI](concepts/robotics/sahi-slicing-inference.md)** slicing at inference → mAP@0.5:0.95 0.760→0.783 on the Roboflow Daycare set. Modest gains (single-camera-bound), useful as a concrete [Ultralytics YOLO](entities/ultralytics-yolo.md) edge-perception pipeline. (2026-02-11)
- [YOLO: You Only Look Once (Redmon et al. 2016)](sources/yolo-you-only-look-once-2016.md) — the paper that founded [single-stage detection](concepts/robotics/single-stage-object-detection.md): detection reframed as **one-pass grid regression** (7×7 grid, no anchors), 63.4% mAP @ 45 fps. More localization error but fewer background false positives; generalizes to artwork. Names its own weakness — **small objects in groups** ("flocks of birds") — the thread's recurring ceiling. (CVPR 2016) (2016-05-09)
- [YOLOv10: Real-Time End-to-End Object Detection (Wang et al. 2024)](sources/yolov10-nms-free-2024.md) — Tsinghua. Removes the last non-end-to-end step, **NMS**, via **consistent dual assignments** (one-to-many + one-to-one heads); YOLOv10-S ~1.8× faster than RT-DETR-R18 at similar AP. The NMS-free idea that becomes default in **YOLO26**. (NeurIPS 2024) (2024-10-30)
- [A Comprehensive Review of YOLO Architectures — v1→v8 + YOLO-NAS (Terven & Cordova-Esparza 2023)](sources/terven-yolo-survey-2023.md) — the most-cited YOLO survey; front-loads the shared **metrics/NMS** machinery, then diffs each version. Doubles as a metrics primer. (2024-02-04)
- [YOLOv1 to YOLOv11 survey (Kotthapalli et al. 2025)](sources/kotthapalli-yolo-survey-2025.md) — extends the lineage to **v11** and documents YOLO's shift from detector to **multi-task vision multi-tool** (segmentation/pose/tracking) + medical/industrial verticals. (2025-08-04)
- [Ultralytics YOLO Evolution — YOLO26/11/8/5 overview (Sapkota & Karkee 2025)](sources/sapkota-ultralytics-yolo-evolution-2025.md) — the current reference for **YOLO26**: DFL removal, native NMS-free, **ProgLoss**, **STAL** (small-target-aware assignment), MuSGD; COCO benchmarks vs v12/v13/RT-DETR/DEIM; deployment/export focus. (2026-03-16)
- [Learning to play Table Tennis using Multi-agent RL (USC project)](sources/usc-table-tennis-marl.md) — semester project: self-play **[MARL](concepts/learning/multi-agent-rl.md)** in a custom **[Unity ML-Agents](entities/unity-ml-agents.md)** ping-pong env; PPO / [SAC](entities/sac.md) / MA-POCA + reward & bat-size curricula; best = SAC self-play (ELO 1200→2352). The wiki's first game-engine-RL + MARL worked example. Unrefereed — numbers illustrative. (ingested 2026-07-17)
- [VLA-0 — Building State-of-the-Art VLAs with Zero Modification](sources/vla-0-paper.md) — NVIDIA (Goyal et al.). **Action-as-text** VLA: an unmodified [Qwen2.5-VL-3B](entities/qwen.md) prints actions as integer strings — no action head, no new tokens, no arch change. With an [ACT](entities/act.md)-ensembling + masked-action recipe it tops [LIBERO](entities/libero.md) (avg 94.7) over π0 / GR00T-N1 / OpenVLA-OFT / SmolVLA **without action pretraining**; +12.5 pts over [SmolVLA](entities/smolvla.md) on real SO-100. Adds a 4th family to the [VLA action-head taxonomy](concepts/learning/vla-models.md). (2025-10-15)
- [TrackNet: Tracking High-speed and Tiny Objects in Sports Applications (Huang et al., NCTU)](sources/tracknet-huang-2019.md) — the founding [TrackNet](entities/tracknet.md) paper: VGG-16 + DeconvNet → Gaussian **[heatmap](concepts/robotics/heatmap-object-localization.md)** instead of bounding boxes, **3 consecutive frames** in. Tennis F1 92.5 (1 frame) → 98.2 (3 frames); locates **occluded** balls from neighbours. Honest caveats: 10-fold CV recall collapses 97.3→75.7, and tennis→badminton transfer gives **35.2 F1**. (2019-07-08)
- [TrackNetV4: Enhancing Fast Sports Object Tracking with Motion Attention Maps (Raj/Wang/Gedeon, ANU)](sources/tracknetv4-motion-attention-2024.md) — plug-and-play **[motion attention](concepts/robotics/motion-attention.md)**: absolute frame differencing → learnable power-normalization "motion prompt" → element-wise fusion into any heatmap tracker. +0.4–0.6 F1 on both TrackNetV2 and V3 at ~zero speed cost (trades precision for recall). Also the source of the **YOLOv7 68.0 vs TrackNetV3 97.5 F1** heatmap-beats-boxes datapoint. Reports its own negative results. (2024-09-22)
- [TrackNetV3 — reference implementation (qaz812345)](sources/tracknetv3-repo.md) — PyTorch, **MIT**, ~276★. Two modules: heatmap tracker (background-estimation input + mixup) + **InpaintNet** trajectory rectification, which treats a broken trajectory as an *inpainting* problem. Shuttlecock 98.56 F1 (recall 94.56→99.33 over V2). The practical modern starting point. (2023-11)
- [TrackNet (weekenddeeplearning) — Keras reimplementation](sources/weekenddeeplearning-tracknet-repo.md) — TrackNetV2-generation in Keras/TF, **GPL-3.0**, pre-trained **tennis + badminton** weights and a bundled **labeling tool** — the asset that matters given TrackNet's failed cross-sport transfer. Framework- and license-incompatible with the V3/V4 line. (ingested 2026-07-21)

- [pinball_tracker GitHub (tanioklyce-dev)](sources/pinball-tracker-repo.md) — **first-party**: the [pinball robot](syntheses/projects/pinball-playing-robot.md)'s fast-loop tracker, actually built. ~1.95M-param heatmap U-Net (not VGG-16) + playfield homography normalization; ~50 FPS desktop. Converged on [heatmap-over-boxes](concepts/robotics/heatmap-object-localization.md) independently of the TrackNet ingest. Refutes two recommendations in the [analysis page](syntheses/projects/fast-ball-tracking-for-robots.md) — classical-CV bootstrap labeling tracks **8%** of frames, and the 10–20k-frame data budget was ~20× too high. **Update 2026-07-22: a third machine scores 0.555 F1** (vs 0.914 on the second) — a real but moderate [TrackNet](entities/tracknet.md) cross-venue penalty, partly un-revising the data-cost finding. First reported as 0.232 (a "collapse") until a **5-frame label-misalignment bug** was caught by watching an overlay video; see the correction in §9. MIT. (2026-07-21, updated 2026-07-22)

- [Introducing Waddle — Agents that Control Robots (Waddle Labs)](sources/waddle-labs-introducing-waddle.md) — **position piece / product announcement** for **[Waddle](entities/waddle-labs.md)**: put an [LLM agent](concepts/agents/llm-agent-architecture.md) on top that decomposes goals, writes **[code as policy](concepts/agents/code-as-policy.md)**, and calls [VLAs](concepts/learning/vla-models.md) as tools — vs. training an end-to-end policy. Three-level skill library (primitives→skills→programs) with cross-agent transfer; an agent autonomously trained an [ACT](entities/act.md) policy from overnight LEGO data. **No success rates / N / CIs** — anecdote-grade against the wiki's [success-rate audit](syntheses/platforms/vla-success-rate-audit.md); its one number (a 6%→32% cursor result) is borrowed from [Anthropic](sources/anthropic-how-claude-performs-on-robotics-tasks.md) and flagged. (2026-07, ingested 2026-08-03)

- [CaP-X: A Framework for Benchmarking and Improving Coding Agents for Robot Manipulation](sources/cap-x-paper.md) — **ICML 2026**, [GEAR](entities/nvidia-gear.md) + Berkeley + Stanford + CMU. The measurement the [code-as-policy](concepts/agents/code-as-policy.md) thread was missing. Separates agent capability from **human-designed API scaffolding** — success rises monotonically as macros are added back, so prior CaP results were substantially carried by their APIs. Four parts: [CaP-Gym](entities/cap-x.md) (187 tasks), CaP-Bench (12 models × 8 tiers, 100 trials/tier), training-free **CaP-Agent0**, and **CaP-RL** (GRPO on the coding agent; a 7B model reaches 84%/76% on a real [Franka](entities/franka-panda.md) from sim-only RL). Beats post-trained VLAs on [LIBERO-PRO](sources/libero-pro-paper.md) under instruction perturbation. Raw pixels as feedback *hurt*; structured text helps. (2026-03-23, v2 2026-07-02, ingested 2026-08-03)
- [ASPIRE: Agentic Skills Discovery for Robotics](sources/aspire-paper.md) — [GEAR](entities/nvidia-gear.md) + UMich + UIUC + Berkeley + CMU; built **on** CaP-X, which is also its baseline. GEAR porting its own **Voyager** skill-library pattern to real manipulation. Three parts: per-primitive **multimodal execution traces** (the ablation's dominant term, 14%→62%), an agent-authored **repair-knowledge** skill library, and evolutionary search (→72%). **+41 to +77 points** over the best baseline on LIBERO-Pro; bimanual handover 20%→92%; zero-shot long-horizon **4%→31%** with success rising in library size. Coding agent is [Claude Opus 4.6](entities/anthropic.md) (1M ctx). Candid limitations: API bounds expressible behavior, library goes stale, **334.9M tokens** for one real-robot task. (2026-06-30, ingested 2026-08-03)

- [allenai/MolmoAct2-SO100_101 — model card](sources/molmoact2-so100-101-model-card.md) — the [MolmoAct2](entities/molmoact2.md) checkpoint fine-tuned for **[SO-100/SO-101](entities/so-arm101.md)** arms: **5B params**, ~24–26 GB float32 / **~16 GB bf16**, **absolute joint-pose control** with annotated language instructions. No benchmarks — it defers to the [paper](sources/molmoact2-paper.md). Its value is the deployment envelope the paper never gave. (2026-05, ingested 2026-08-03)
- [allenai/molmoact2 — GitHub repo](sources/molmoact2-github-repo.md) — the MolmoAct2 codebase. **Ships as a [LeRobot](entities/lerobot.md) application** (v3.0 datasets, LeRobot submodule, LeRobot training workflows); full checkpoint family incl. DROID/YAM/SO-100_101/LIBERO variants; FastAPI client-server deployment; [ManiSkill](entities/maniskill.md) sim eval; **no Jetson support of any kind**. Ai2's porting advice: start from the *nearest embodiment's* checkpoint, not the generalist base. Apache 2.0. (2026-05, ingested 2026-08-03)
- [Gemini Robotics — DeepMind model page](sources/deepmind-gemini-robotics-model-page.md) — **version discovery**: the family has moved to a **"2" generation** the wiki did not have — **Gemini Robotics 2** (VLA), **ER 2** (embodied reasoning, public preview in AI Studio), and a structurally new **On-Device 2** ("optimized to run locally on robotic hardware"). Claims whole-body humanoid control, dexterity, multi-robot collaboration, and "any bi-arm robot in just a few hours" (~200 examples per coverage — a *fine-tuning* claim, the disciplined cousin of [Waddle](entities/waddle-labs.md)'s zero-shot version). Partners: [Agile Robots](entities/agile-robots.md), [Apptronik](entities/apptronik-apollo.md), [Boston Dynamics](entities/boston-dynamics.md). **No numbers, no dates on the page**; a blog post and safety tech report exist and are [backlogged](backlog.md). (2026-07-30, ingested 2026-08-03)

- [Gemini Robotics 2 brings whole body intelligence to robots (DeepMind blog)](sources/gemini-robotics-2-blog.md) — the **substantive GR 2 announcement**, with the numbers the [model page](sources/deepmind-gemini-robotics-model-page.md) lacked. Whole-body pick-up by height (shelf **76.3%** / table 68.4% / floor **45.7%**); gripper dexterity on Franka Duo (precise insertion **89.6%**); and **candid multi-finger numbers** on [SharpaWave](entities/sharpa-wave.md) — unscrew bulb **92%** but screw bulb **36%**, dustpan 32%, ziplock 40%. **Answers the wiki's open question: the dexterity ceiling lifted for grippers, not for fingers.** On-Device 2 adapts to new embodiments in a few hours / **<200 examples**. **[SO101](entities/so-arm101.md) is a listed platform.** No trial counts, no baselines. (2026-07-30)
- [Gemini Robotics 2: Safety Evaluations](sources/gemini-robotics-2-safety-report.md) — introduces **ASIMOV-Agentic** (HF, CC-BY-4.0), benchmarking the *routing* decision: delegate to the VLA, ask the human, or fire a safety tool. Safety tool calling **100%** (ER 2 / Claude Opus 4.8 / GPT 5.5); VLA feasibility filtering **62.0% → 95.8%** with richer training-distribution summaries; real Apollo 2 safe-stopping **99%/96%**. **But no model reaches an acceptable human-proximity operating point** — FPR <5% implies **FNR >40%**. DeepMind's own conclusion: use them "alongside deterministic, low-level safety guardrails," and the report **explicitly excludes the functional-safety architecture**. Also the wiki's best ISO map (10218:2025, 13482, forthcoming **25785-1**, 13855, 13849-1, IEC 60204-1). (2026-07-29)

- [π0.5: a VLA with Open-World Generalization](sources/pi-zero-5-paper.md) — **the wiki's most-cited secondhand model finally has its primary** ([Physical Intelligence](entities/physical-intelligence.md), Apr 2025). Co-training on heterogeneous data — **97.6% of pre-training is not mobile-manipulation data** — yields the first end-to-end system cleaning kitchens/bedrooms in **entirely unseen homes** (10–15 min tasks); at 104 training locations it matches a model trained on the test homes. Hierarchical inference (subtask → actions); the **discrete-FAST-pretrain → flow-matching-post-train recipe** that [KI](sources/knowledge-insulation-paper.md) and [MolmoAct2](entities/molmoact2.md) later adopt. Read against the 2026 record: **co-training bought scene generalization, not instruction generalization.** (2025-04-22, ingested 2026-08-03)

- [SciFi-Benchmark (Sermanet et al.)](sources/scifi-benchmark-paper.md) — the last un-ingested work on [DeepMind's safety page](sources/deepmind-gemini-robotics-safety-page.md); the provenance of ASIMOV-Dilemmas-Scifi. 824 sci-fi works → 9,056 questions via LLM introspection. Constitutions lift alignment **79.4% → 95.8%** and resist adversarial prompting (**23.3% → 92.3%**); sci-fi-derived constitutions top-perform on [ASIMOV](entities/asimov-benchmark.md)'s real-world data. Fun headline: sci-fi's own agents align at only 21.2%. (2025-03-13, ingested 2026-08-03)
- [allenai/vla-evaluation-harness](sources/vla-evaluation-harness-github.md) — Ai2's **any-VLA × 18-benchmark** evaluation infrastructure (47× throughput; 2,000 LIBERO episodes ≈ 18 min/H100). Supports **LIBERO-Pro with MolmoAct2 / GR00T N1.7 / π0.5** — the wiki's most consequential open question is **no longer blocked on tooling**. Reproduction reports independently verify four LeRobot checkpoints at 96–100% of published LIBERO scores. (2026-07, ingested 2026-08-03)
- [Gemini Robotics On-Device 2 — model card](sources/gemini-robotics-on-device-2-model-card.md) — partially closes the On-Device-2 envelope item: built on GR 1.5 tech + **on-device Gemma**; **SO101 53.3% (v2) vs 6.7% (v1)**, Dexmate 75.6% vs 33.3%; limitation: OOD + "controlling high-degree-of-freedom robots." **Still no params / memory / hardware / rate.** Trusted Testers only. (2026-07-30, ingested 2026-08-03)

- [MolmoAct: Action Reasoning Models that can Reason in Space](sources/molmoact-paper.md) — **the MolmoAct v1 primary**, closing the Ai2 line's last secondhand gap ([Ai2](entities/ai2.md)+UW, Aug 2025). Three independently-decodable reasoning stages: **depth tokens → visual trace → actions**, with **steering by editing the trace**. Robots: **Franka only** (single-arm on a DROID-style mobile mount + bimanual); released the 10,689-trajectory MolmoAct Dataset. SimplerEnv zero-shot **70.5%**; real-world **+10/+22.7%** over π0-FAST; early arena-style **Elo** policy evaluation predating [RoboArena](entities/roboarena.md). (2025-08-11, ingested 2026-08-03)

- [Reconstruction or Semantics? What Makes a Latent Space Useful for Robotic World Models](sources/latent-space-robotic-world-models-paper.md) — Mila / Chandar Lab. **Runs a JEPA representation through the policy-evaluator role** — OpenVLA-7B rolled out inside six world models that differ *only* in their frozen encoder, on real Bridge V2. Semantic latents roughly **double** VLA-in-the-loop success over VAE latents (**0.362 vs 0.169**), with a wider OOD margin (0.575 vs 0.287). [V-JEPA 2.1](entities/v-jepa-2.md) strongest overall. At larger DiT scale, VAE/Cosmos close the *policy* gap but not the **action-recovery** gap — **scale buys the parts that go through pixels, not the parts that go through dynamics.** Contradicts the SNU probe study on whether image-SSL (Web-DINO, SigLIP 2) belongs with V-JEPA. (2026-05, ingested 2026-08-08)
- [VP²: A Control-Centric Benchmark for Video Prediction](sources/vp2-paper.md) — Tian, [Finn](entities/chelsea-finn.md) & Wu, **ICLR 2023**. **The founding result the whole 2026 evaluation literature rediscovers.** Perceptual metrics mis-rank video predictors for control, and the correlation's **sign is task-dependent** — the best FVD in the study (4.9) scores **10%** control success, the worst (51.7) scores **80%**; SSIM varies 0.2 pts while success ranges 32%→82%. 11 task categories / 310 instances, full MPC stack included, **one-function model interface**, and a **simulator-as-model upper bound**. Also: capacity 6M→300M buys **no** control gain, data plateaus early, and diffusion cost 220 s vs FitVid's 5.63 s per 10-frame pass. (2023-04, ingested 2026-08-08)
- [What Makes Video World Model Latents Action-Relevant: Prediction over Reconstruction](sources/action-relevant-latents-paper.md) — Seoul National University. **The first instrument that scores JEPA and pixel models on one axis**: a frozen-feature inverse-dynamics probe across **eight encoder families** on [LIBERO](entities/libero.md) task-OOD. **Pixel fidelity and action recoverability are orthogonal** — at ~20 dB PSNR action R² spans −0.01 to +0.46, and the highest-PSNR backbones (SDXL VAE, Cosmos-1 tokenizer) score *lowest*. [V-JEPA 2](entities/v-jepa-2.md) 0.40→**0.85** under a small ID loss; VideoMAE 0.75; Web-DINO **0.16**. So **temporal video pretraining is the big lever and the JEPA objective is worth ~+0.10** — real, smaller than advertised. Advantage concentrated on **rotation**; action signal **peaks at layer 14 and decays to layer 22**, so final-layer features are near the trunk's worst point. (2026-06, ingested 2026-08-08)
- [Latent Video Prediction Learns Better World Models](sources/latent-video-prediction-better-world-models-paper.md) — Melbourne + Monash; 1,000+ A100-hours. Names the gap: world-model evaluation is "largely confined to a single top-1 accuracy score on clean benchmarks." Four **matched-capacity ViT-L** models across five robustness axes. **Stable features are not usable features** — VideoPrism holds representational similarity **>0.98** under patch dropout while collapsing to **2.7%** top-1; V-JEPA 2.1 retains **46.1%**. Latent predictors lead 5 of 6 corruption types, uniquely encode the **arrow of time**, and spot pretend actions best where the cue is the *absence* of contact. Caveat: SSv2 action classification, **not control**. (2026-05, ingested 2026-08-08)
- [WorldArena: A Unified Benchmark for Evaluating Perception and Functional Utility of Embodied World Models](sources/worldarena-paper.md) — Tsinghua-led, 8 institutions. **The instrument the [HAI brief](sources/hai-world-model-spatial-intelligence-brief.md) said didn't exist.** Scores 14 world models on perception *and* on the three roles they get used in. **The perception–functionality gap, quantified: EWMScore correlates r = 0.825 with human judgment, 0.600 with data-engine utility, and 0.360 with action planning.** No model beats real data as a data engine; every model loses to [π0.5](entities/pi-zero-5.md) as a planner by 3–4×; learned policy evaluators **inflate** absolute success rates ("partial overfitting to successful trajectories") while [Ctrl-World](entities/ctrl-world.md) still ranks at r = 0.986. [Genie Envisioner](entities/genie-envisioner.md) comes **last of 14**. (2026-02, ingested 2026-08-08)
- [WorldArena 2.0: Extending Embodied World Model Benchmarking on Modality, Functionality and Platform](sources/worldarena-2-paper.md) — same group + Stanford (Wetzstein). Adds **visuotactile** (UniVTAC; plug-in tactile VAE), world model **as an online RL environment** — the one role where they work, closing ~⅔ of the gap to simulator-RL — and **real-robot** evaluation on an AgileX [ALOHA](entities/aloha.md). Perceptual rankings transfer across platforms; **functional rankings collapse against a real robot.** (2026-05, ingested 2026-08-08)
- [WorldRoamBench: An Open-World Benchmark for Long-Horizon Stability of Interactive World Models](sources/worldroambench-paper.md) — AMAP CV Lab (Alibaba) + Nanjing/Tsinghua/PKU. **The wiki's only measured numbers on [Genie 3](entities/genie-3.md)**, from a competitor: 1st of 10 in FPV (73.81), winning on **memory** while placing 7th on action following. 600+ cases, 10–60 s continuous WASD. Four new metrics because the old ones measured the wrong thing — trajectory scores above 85 hide **sub-65% per-frame** action accuracy; "high visual quality ≠ good action following"; and **stricter physics adherence may cost you action-following points**, because refusing to clip through a wall means leaving the prescribed path. (2026-07, ingested 2026-08-08)
- [HAI Issue Brief — The World Model and Spatial Intelligence Era: Governing AI Beyond Language](sources/hai-world-model-spatial-intelligence-brief.md) — [Stanford HAI](entities/stanford-hai.md), nine authors incl. [Fei-Fei Li](entities/fei-fei-li.md). **The wiki's first prescriptive policy document.** Foundation-model governance asks *what does it generate*, agentic governance asks *what may it do* — world models add a third object: **is the learned environment valid for its intended use?** A world model's error is "a counterfeit of physical reality that can look flawless while being wrong," inherited silently by everything trained, certified, or decided inside it. Carries the **renderer / simulator / planner** [functional taxonomy](concepts/world-models/world-model-functional-taxonomy.md) (planners least mature) and its own kill-switch — *thresholds per category are gamed or outgrown; safeguards attach to **deployment context**.* Scarce input is **action-labeled interaction data**, unscrapeable. Names the benchmark patchwork (VBench/VideoPhy/PhyGenBench/WorldScore/WorldModelBench/**WorldArena**/[LIBERO](entities/libero.md)) and finds none adequate for safety-critical deployment. (2026-07, ingested 2026-08-07)
- [Nav2 Behavior Trees documentation](sources/nav2-behavior-trees-docs.md) — **the production instantiation of the BT formalism**, on a very large number of real robots. [Nav2](entities/nav2.md) runs [BehaviorTree.CPP](entities/behaviortree-cpp.md) v4 and ships `navigate_to_pose_w_replanning_and_recovery.xml` (full XML captured). **Nav2 had to invent control nodes the classical vocabulary lacks** — `PipelineSequence` (re-ticks prior children so the planner replans while the controller follows), `RecoveryNode`, `RoundRobin` (retains position → escalating recovery), `NonblockingSequence` — and **almost every addition is about *when* things tick, not control flow**. Two-tier **cause-selected** recovery gated by `WouldA…RecoveryHelp` error codes; goal change preempts recovery; runtime plugin swap via the blackboard. **Qualifies the [guardrails](syntheses/agents/guardrails-for-robot-agents.md) finding: an execution rail *does* ship — scoped to navigation.** (ingested 2026-08-04)
- [BehaviorTree.CPP documentation (v4)](sources/behaviortree-cpp-docs.md) — the MIT-licensed C++17 engine, v4.9. **Retires the [book](sources/behavior-trees-book.md)'s "BT tools are less mature" disadvantage.** Asynchronous actions first-class, runtime XML trees, node plugins, **type-safe ports + blackboard** (*"custom TreeNodes… are not conceptually different from **functions**"*), logging/replay, Groot2 editor. v3→v4 renames captured. **The port model is the missing interface for a [BT-over-VLA](concepts/robotics/behavior-trees.md)**: a typed function-call boundary around an opaque policy. Note: `behaviortree.dev` 403s unauthenticated fetchers. (ingested 2026-08-04)
- [Behavior Trees in Robotics and AI: An Introduction](sources/behavior-trees-book.md) — [Colledanchise](entities/michele-colledanchise.md) & [Ögren](entities/petter-ogren.md), 198-pp book. **The dominant *deployed* action-composition formalism, absent from this wiki until now.** Six node types (Sequence `→`, Fallback `?`, Parallel, Decorator, Action, Condition) ticked from the root, returning Running/Success/Failure. Formally **generalizes** FSMs, HFSMs, subsumption, teleo-reactive programs, and decision trees; modularity comes from **two-way** control transfer where an FSM transition is a one-way `goto`. Also: safety-by-construction, state-space proofs, **stochastic BTs → Markov chains** yielding success probability and expected completion time, PA-BT planning, and GP/RL/LfD for BTs. Candidly lists when BTs *don't* help. **Founds [behavior trees](concepts/robotics/behavior-trees.md).** (2018; arXiv v6 2022, ingested 2026-08-04)
- [RT-1: Robotics Transformer for Real-World Control at Scale](sources/rt-1-paper.md) — Brohan et al. (Robotics at Google + [Everyday Robots](entities/everyday-robots.md)). **The ancestor of the entire VLA lineage, finally primary.** 35M params at **3 Hz** (FiLM-EfficientNet-B3 + TokenLearner, identity-initialized FiLM); **130k demos / 700+ tasks / 13 robots / 17 months**; seen **97**, unseen **76** vs Gato 65/52 and BC-Z 72/19 — baselines **retrained on RT-1's data**. BC-Z XL is *worse* than BC-Z. Absorbs sim data at no cost and **Kuka IIWA** data for **22 → 39%** on new bin-picking — the wiki's earliest cross-embodiment datapoint. (2022-12, v2 2023-08, ingested 2026-08-04)
- [RT-2: Vision-Language-Action Models Transfer Web Knowledge to Robotic Control](sources/rt-2-paper.md) — **the paper that coined "VLA."** [PaLI-X](entities/pali-x.md) 55B / PaLM-E 12B; actions as text tokens; **co-fine-tuning** on web + robot data is the load-bearing choice (precursor to [π0.5](entities/pi-zero-5.md) co-training and [KI](concepts/learning/knowledge-insulation.md)).
