- [OmniGibson](entities/omnigibson.md) — Stanford's physics+rendering sim (Omniverse + PhysX 5) underneath BEHAVIOR-1K; **deformables + fluids + extended states** (temperature/soaked/cooked) most sims lack; iGibson successor; needs [Isaac Sim](entities/nvidia-isaac-sim.md) 4.1.0; 14 robots (incl. [Galaxea R1](entities/galaxea-r1.md)/R1Pro, [Stretch](entities/stretch.md), [Tiago](entities/tiago.md), [Franka](entities/franka-panda.md)); OpenAI Gym API. (2 sources)
- [flybody](entities/flybody.md) — HHMI Janelia + Google DeepMind anatomically detailed *Drosophila* body in MuJoCo (102 DoFs, walking + flight); Apache-2.0. (7 sources)
- [NeuroMechFly](entities/neuromechfly.md) — NeLy/EPFL *Drosophila* body sim with vision + olfaction + brain–VNC hierarchy; v2 (Wang-Chen 2024); flygym v2.x.x package actively maintained 2026 with Warp/MJWarp GPU acceleration. Apache-2.0. (4 sources)

### RL API standards
- [Gymnasium](entities/gymnasium.md) — single-agent RL env API; OpenAI gym successor under Farama. (5 sources)
- [PettingZoo](entities/pettingzoo.md) — multi-agent RL env API. (2 sources)

### Robot platforms
- [Koch v1.1](entities/koch-v1-1.md) — the **Dynamixel-tier leader–follower teleop pair** and one of [LeRobot](entities/lerobot.md)'s 8 native platforms; Jess Moss's assembly-focused revision of Alexander Koch's original. Repo BOM confirms the wiki's "~€670" figure is the **pair** ($199 leader + $278 follower US): 6× XL330-M077 on the leader, 2× XL430-W250 + 4× XL330-M288 on the follower. The ~3× premium over [SO-ARM101](entities/so-arm101.md) is why FeeTech came to dominate community datasets. **Dormant since September 2024** — historical price reference, not a live recommendation. (1 source)
- [Open Duck Mini](entities/open-duck-mini.md) — **[Antoine Pirrone](entities/pollen-robotics.md)'s ~35 cm 3D-printable BDX-droid miniature** (Apache-2.0, 3.5k stars) and the documented ancestor of [Microduck](entities/microduck.md) — Pollen's own `policies/README.md` names his personal repo as the provenance of the shipped ONNX policies. Dynamixel **XC330** legs, policies on a **Raspberry Pi Zero 2W**. Training lineage runs Isaac Gym/AWD → [MuJoCo Playground](entities/mujoco-playground.md) → [mjlab](entities/mjlab.md), with Rhoban **BAM** actuator ID throughout. Google ran [Gemma 4](entities/gemma4.md) E2B on-device on two of these at I/O 2026 — as a **conversational head; the ducks did not walk**. Repo self-describes as *"a work in progress"* with *"too much play at some joints"* — the defect its successor now simulates as ±1° backlash. (2 sources)
- [Microduck](entities/microduck.md) — [Pollen](entities/pollen-robotics.md)/[HF](entities/hugging-face.md)'s **$399, 25 cm, sub-800 g RL biped** (pre-orders 2026-08-27). 15 motors ([Dynamixel](entities/dynamixel.md) XL330 class), **RK3566 / 1 GB RAM**, 50 Hz ONNX loop, camera + **8×8 ToF** (marketed as LiDAR) + 2 IMUs + grasping beak + NFC, NP-F550 camera battery. Seven shipped policies, all retrainable, trained in [mjlab](entities/mjlab.md) with a **voltage-level actuator model and simulated backlash**. The cheapest legged RL platform in this wiki, and the only robot here whose full sim-to-real recipe ships with the product. Fall recovery doubles as **reset automation**; four units = $1,596 [multi-agent](concepts/learning/multi-agent-rl.md) testbed, though nothing shipped lets the robots perceive each other. Software Apache-2.0; **not open-source hardware**. (1 source)
- [reBot Arm B601](entities/rebot-arm-b601.md) — [Seeed](entities/seeed-studio.md)'s own **6+1-DOF open-source CAN-bus benchtop arm**, two variants: **DM** ([Damiao](entities/damiao.md), 767 mm / 1.5 kg / 0.2 mm / 24 V, **$1,499**) and **RS** ([Robstride](entities/robstride.md), 754 mm / 2.5 kg / <0.1 mm / 48 V). ROS 1/2 + MoveIt + LeRobot + Isaac Sim + Pinocchio; ships a full [NVIDIA DLI sim-to-real course](sources/seeed-nvidia-dli-rebot-sim-to-real-course.md). The wiki's first entry between the FeeTech hobby tier and $20k research arms. LeRobot support is via **forks**, not the plugin convention. Backdrivability unmeasured; no published VLA success rates. (2 sources)
- [Star Arm 102](entities/star-arm-102.md) — Seeed's **$200 6+1-DOF leader arm** for teleoperating the reBot B601. Claims **Pieper-criterion** compliance, i.e. closed-form analytical IK — the property that keeps a 30 Hz teleop recording loop deterministic. Drives the real and the Isaac-Sim follower from the same rig. (2 sources)
- [UME (Universal Manipulation Exoskeleton)](entities/ume.md) — **$1,900** torque-feedback teleoperation exoskeleton (Ant Group + Stanford); records **joint torques**, retargets to OpenArm / [Franka](entities/franka-panda.md) / [X-ARM](entities/xarm-7.md), IMU for mobile use. Evaluated on a **$9,533** bimanual holonomic mobile manipulator. **Contact-rich policies from 26–157 demos each.** The researched answer to the position-only recording gap; [OpenFT](entities/openft-sensor.md) is the cheap unmaintained one. No success rates published. (1 source)
- [Tien Kung (X-Humanoid)](entities/tien-kung.md) — Beijing Innovation Center humanoid with **dual dexterous hands**; 15,187 trajectories in [RoboMIND](entities/robomind.md). The wiki's clearest case of **dexterous-hand exclusion** from cross-embodiment learning — [X-VLA](entities/x-vla.md) consumes RoboMIND and drops this split entirely. No specs published. (1 source) _stub_
- [Sourccey](entities/sourccey.md) — [Vulcan Robotics](entities/vulcan-robotics.md)'s open-source household robot (Aug 2026); 1,030 mm / 15.88 kg / 4× mecanum / 2× 5-DOF [FeeTech](entities/feetech.md) arms / [Pi 5](entities/raspberry-pi-5.md) / 2D LiDAR / **12 V 10 Ah LiFePO4 (~120 Wh)** / 7-inch touchscreen / Oculus IK teleop. **First product here to ship a policy** ([X-VLA](entities/x-vla.md) laundry folding). Payload ≈0.3–0.5 kg (wiki estimate); no encoders, no force sensing, no URDF, no price. (2 sources)
- [NORI A3](entities/nori-a3.md) — [Nori Robotics](entities/nori-robotics.md)'s **$1,688** bimanual wheeled home manipulator (Fall 2026). **Two 7+1-DOF arms** — a redundant arm at a price where the rest of this tier is 5-DoF ([SO-101](entities/so-arm101.md), [Sourccey](entities/sourccey.md)) or 6-DoF ([Piper](entities/agilex-piper.md)) — but 1.5 kg payload each, **four RGB cameras and a planar LiDAR with no depth at manipulation range**, and a **[Pi 5](entities/raspberry-pi-5.md) 4 GB** as the entire onboard computer, which makes the "Nori Lab" laptop app the actual brain. Height, mass, reach and base type unpublished. (2 sources)
- [AgileX Piper](entities/agilex-piper.md) — 6-DoF lightweight research arm; the real-world evaluation platform in [TurboVLA](entities/turbovla.md) (wrist + third-person RealSense D435, 65 demos/task), and the "low-cost robot" in [GraspGen-X](sources/graspgenx-paper.md) (100%/10 trials — but 2 objects with **known meshes** and complete point clouds). Now the wiki's most-measured affordable arm for grasping, from two directions that never meet: [RoboTwin](entities/robotwin.md)'s problem is **arm** DoF, GraspGen-X's is **gripper** morphology. Price/payload/reach undocumented here. (2 sources)
- [Franka Panda](entities/franka-panda.md) — 7-DOF research-grade arm; default tabletop manipulator across DROID, V-JEPA 2, JEPA-WMs, RUM, Diffusion Policy, UMI, Huh-et-al-accessibility, Anthropic's robotics eval. (30 sources)
- [Kinova Jaco](entities/kinova-jaco.md) — commercial wheelchair-mounted assistive arm (2010, ~$35k, medical device, insurance-reimbursed in DE/NL); 90 cm reach, 1.6 kg payload, Cartesian control via wheelchair drive controls; research editions carry the Nanavati feeding work. (2 sources)
- [xArm 7](entities/xarm-7.md) — UFactory commercial 7-DOF arm; secondary tabletop manipulator; RUM cross-embodiment transfer target (~10pt drop vs Stretch). (2 sources) _stub_
- [Sharpa Wave hand](entities/sharpa-wave.md) — 22-DoF anthropomorphic dexterous hand; primary post-training target on Galaxea R1Pro in [EgoScale](sources/egoscale-paper.md). Joint-space control. (1 source) _stub_
- [TurtleBot](entities/turtlebot.md) — canonical educational ROS mobile robot (4 generations); TurtleBot 4 in education, TurtleBot 2i used in DRAGON 2024 assistive navigation; Rosetta ships `turtlebot3.yaml` as the reference mobile-base contract. (6 sources)
- [iRobot Create 3](entities/irobot-create-3.md) — Roomba-i3-derived ROS 2 mobile-robot base; chassis under [TurtleBot 4](entities/turtlebot.md). (1 source) _stub_
- [Tiago](entities/tiago.md) — PAL Robotics dual-arm mobile manipulator; ROS-native; used in Huh et al. 2026 accessibility study. (3 sources)
- [YAM (i2RT)](entities/yam.md) — 6-DOF CAN-bus tabletop arm for embodied-AI data collection ($2,999–$4,999); supplied [GR00T N1.6](sources/groot-n1_6.md) teleop data and is [MolmoAct2](entities/molmoact2.md)'s flagship platform (the 720-hr open BimanualYAM dataset; <$6,000 rig). (5 sources)
- [Galaxea R1](entities/galaxea-r1.md) — wheeled dual-arm humanoid (24 DOF; 5 kg; 550 TOPS); a supported [OmniGibson](entities/omnigibson.md) robot (R1/R1Pro); simulated R1 Pro on BEHAVIOR is a [GR00T N1.6](sources/groot-n1_6.md) data embodiment. (3 sources)
- [Unitree Go2](entities/unitree-go2.md) — cheap-tier quadruped "robot dog" (~$1.6k Air → ~$17k EDU); onboard lidar + camera. The robot in [Project Fetch](sources/anthropic-project-fetch-robot-dog.md) **per secondary coverage only** — Anthropic's post never names it. Cheap-tier counterpart to [Spot](entities/spot.md). **Identification confirmed** by the [robotics eval](sources/anthropic-how-claude-performs-on-robotics-tasks.md) (12-DoF; simulated + real). The wiki's best-instrumented quadruped. (3 sources)
- [Yuri](entities/yuri.md) ([Sensori Robotics](entities/sensori-robotics.md)) — integrated bimanual manipulator (2× 7-DOF OpenArm+; Jetson AGX Orin 64 GB); Desktop (tabletop) + Mobile (wheeled OpenBase); bilateral force-feedback teleop out-of-box; LeRobot recording + GR00T/π0/X-VLA/SmolVLA support; research-tier, quote-only. (1 source)
- [Zeroth M1](entities/zeroth-m1.md) ([Zeroth Robotics](entities/zeroth-robotics.md)) — $2,499 consumer home robot; 494 mm, 2.8 kg, 20 DoF, biped 0.05 m/s + wheeled 0.6 m/s, ~2 h endurance; markets fall detection and elder safety with no published evidence, compute spec, or privacy statement. Social/monitoring tier, not mobile manipulation. (1 source)

### Humanoids
- [Agile ONE](entities/agile-one.md) — [Agile Robots](entities/agile-robots.md) industrial humanoid; 174 cm / 69 kg / 20 kg payload / 2 m/s; **21-DOF dexterous hands** with per-joint force-torque sensing; built in Bavaria, series production 2026; part of an integrated AMR+arm+vision stack, not a standalone product. (1 source)
- [EngineAI T800](entities/engineai-t800.md) — [EngineAI](entities/engineai.md) heavy-duty flagship humanoid; 173 cm / 75 kg / **450 N·m** self-developed joints / **world-first solid-state battery** (4–5 hr) / 360° LiDAR; the [URKL](sources/urkl-robot-combat-league.md) combat platform (side kicks, 360° aerial rotations). (2 sources)
- [Atlas](entities/atlas.md) — Boston Dynamics flagship; closed development; capability-bar humanoid. (1 source) _stub_
- [Fourier GR-1](entities/fourier-gr-1.md) — Fourier Intelligence humanoid; NVIDIA GEAR's de-facto humanoid testbed — primary real-robot platform for [GR00T N1](sources/groot-n1-paper.md) (88 h teleop + all real-world evals) and primary OOD eval target across [DreamDojo](sources/dreamdojo-paper.md). (6 sources)
- [Spot](entities/spot.md) — Boston Dynamics' commercial quadruped; the BD-platform-with-an-API; documented Gemini Robotics-ER 1.5 integration. (1 source)
- [Tesla Optimus](entities/tesla-optimus.md) — Tesla's vertically-integrated humanoid; closed development. (0 sources) _stub_
- [Foundation Robotics (Phantom)](entities/foundation-robotics.md) — humanoid for **military/industrial** work (1.8 m / 80 kg / 29 DoF), and the wiki's sharpest **claims-record** page. Its product page states payload as **both 40 kg and 20 kg** in the same block; the company describes its differentiating actuator as **"proprietary cycloid"** on the page and as **"rolling contact gearboxes rather than… cycloid drives"** in a press briefing; and GM denied its investment/purchase-order claims outright — *"never had an agreement of any kind."* Publishes no battery, runtime, compute, sensor or price data. `[live-web]` throughout; **nothing ingested**. (0 sources)
- [Figure](entities/figure.md) — Figure AI's humanoid line (01/02/03) + Helix VLA; BMW pilots. Now the line-and-company page; per-generation detail split out below. **Corrected 2026-08-28**: Figure 02's mass was carried here as ~60 kg and is **~67 kg** (Figure 03 is 61 kg and 9% lighter). (9 sources)
- [Figure 03](entities/figure-03.md) — Figure AI's third-generation humanoid (2025-10-09), and the **best-documented closed-tier humanoid in the wiki** — not because Figure publishes results (it publishes none) but because it publishes *manufacturing*. **5'8" (~173 cm) / 61 kg / 20 kg payload / 5 h / 1.2 m/s / 2.3 kWh**; palm cameras + **3 g fingertip tactile**; 2 kW charging through the feet; washable soft goods; **350+ built at one per hour**. Figure publishes **no DOF, no onboard compute, no price**, and the widely-quoted 168 cm / 40 DOF / 4 h figures have no primary behind them. (6 sources)
- [1X NEO](entities/1x-neo.md) — household humanoid; 22 hand DOF/side; Redwood AI VLM; 22 dB; $200 deposit. (2 sources)
- [Apptronik Apollo](entities/apptronik-apollo.md) — UT Austin spinout; NVIDIA-aligned ([GR00T](entities/nvidia-groot.md) target); Mercedes-Benz pilots; one of [Gemini Robotics 1.5](sources/gemini-robotics-1-5-report.md)'s three training embodiments. (2 sources) _stub_
- [Digit](entities/digit.md) — Agility Robotics; **first commercially-deployed humanoid** (GXO, Amazon); now the **inaugural [NVIDIA Halos](entities/nvidia-halos.md)** humanoid (onboard IGX Thor + Halos Core, Inside-Out safety). (2 sources)
- [Unitree H1](entities/unitree-h1.md) — Chinese affordable research humanoid (~$90k); rapid 2024–2026 academic adoption. (0 sources) _stub_
- [Tairan He](entities/tairan-he.md) — CMU/NVIDIA; **co-first author of four of the five** humanoid WBC papers here ([H2O](sources/h2o-paper.md), [OmniH2O](sources/omnih2o-paper.md), [HOVER](sources/hover-paper.md), [ASAP](sources/asap-paper.md)). The humanoid branch's through-line author, as [Pathak](entities/deepak-pathak.md) is the quadruped branch's. (4 sources)
- [AMASS](entities/amass.md) — the human mocap archive nearly all learned humanoid WBC is trained on, via retargeting. **Not a drop-in robot dataset**: [H2O](sources/h2o-paper.md) must delete motions the robot cannot physically do (cartwheels, over-long steps) and [OmniH2O](sources/omnih2o-paper.md) must deliberately skew the distribution toward standing/squatting. Both treat that curation as a headline contribution. (2 sources)
- [Ashish Kumar](entities/ashish-kumar.md) — UC Berkeley; **first author of [RMA](sources/rma-paper.md)** and co-first author of [egocentric-vision locomotion](sources/egocentric-vision-locomotion-paper.md). Co-authored **both** instances of the engineered privileged-teacher approach and neither paper that abandons it. (2 sources)
- [Ananye Agarwal](entities/ananye-agarwal.md) — founding researcher at [Skild AI](entities/skild-ai.md); CMU PhD under [Pathak](entities/deepak-pathak.md). Co-first author of [egocentric-vision locomotion](sources/egocentric-vision-locomotion-paper.md) (2022) and co-author of [LocoFormer](sources/locoformer-paper.md) (2025) — author at both ends of the arc from engineered privileged distillation to long-context generalism. MIT TR Innovator Under 35 (APAC). (2 sources)
- [Jetson Xavier NX](entities/jetson-xavier-nx.md) — Volta-generation predecessor of the [Orin NX](entities/jetson-orin-nx.md) in the same 69.6 × 45 mm / 260-pin SO-DIMM slot; 21 TOPS @ 15 W. The onboard compute for [egocentric-vision locomotion](sources/egocentric-vision-locomotion-paper.md)'s 50 Hz policy. **Specs are live-web and explicitly not decision-grade** — no datasheet ingested. (1 source)
- [Isaac Gym](entities/isaac-gym.md) — NVIDIA's GPU-physics simulator that made massively parallel locomotion RL practical; **deprecated**, superseded by [Isaac Lab](entities/nvidia-isaac-lab.md). Its cheap-physics/expensive-rendering asymmetry is why the locomotion corpus is full of privileged-teacher distillation. (1 source)
- [legged_gym](entities/legged-gym.md) — ETH RSL's locomotion training library on Isaac Gym (Rudin et al., CoRL 2021): ANYmal walking in **under 4 min flat / ~20 min rough** on one GPU. Its **terrain curriculum** — promote on traversing half the length, demote on failure — propagated into downstream work including [egocentric-vision locomotion](sources/egocentric-vision-locomotion-paper.md). (1 source)
- [Unitree A1](entities/unitree-a1.md) — 12 kg low-cost quadruped; the workhorse of learned locomotion research and a fixed point across this wiki's coverage: [RMA](sources/rma-paper.md)'s 2021 deployment platform and one of [LocoFormer](sources/locoformer-paper.md)'s 2025 zero-shot targets (0.92). (2 sources)
- [Unitree G1](entities/unitree-g1.md) — smaller, cheaper Unitree (~$16k); cheapest serious humanoid platform; the [GR00T](entities/nvidia-groot.md) line's main cross-embodiment / whole-body target ([N1.5](sources/groot-n1_5.md) 98.8% seen; [GEAR-SONIC](entities/gear-sonic.md) whole-body controller, G1-only). Now the **de-facto shared benchmark for learned [whole-body control](concepts/robotics/whole-body-control.md)** — [SONIC](sources/sonic-paper.md), [MotionBricks](sources/motionbricks-paper.md), [BumbleBee](sources/bumblebee-experts-to-generalist-wbc.md), and [WBC-AGILE](sources/wbc-agile-github.md) all target it. Also **the hardest platform** in Anthropic's [robotics eval](sources/anthropic-how-claude-performs-on-robotics-tasks.md) — trained WBC policies hit 99.2% sim-to-real on it while no frontier LLM could stand it up from the floor even once. (12 sources)
- [Booster T1](entities/booster-t1.md) — bipedal humanoid (Booster Robotics); [WBC-AGILE](sources/wbc-agile-github.md)'s second sim-to-real validation platform alongside G1. (1 source) _stub_
- [NAO](entities/nao.md) — SoftBank/Aldebaran 58-cm educational humanoid; canonical since 2008. (0 sources) _stub_
- [TonyPi](entities/tonypi.md) — Hiwonder hobby-tier biped kit ($300–700); educational-tier sibling of [ROSOrin Pro](entities/rosorin-pro.md). (0 sources) _stub_
- [Stretch](entities/stretch.md) — Hello Robot's mobile manipulator (Stretch 3 / Stretch 4). De-facto research platform; natively supported in LeRobot. (25 sources)
- [Mobile ALOHA](entities/aloha.md) — Stanford bimanual mobile manipulator with whole-body teleop; 4× [ViperX 300](entities/viperx-300.md) + AgileX Tracer base; $32k incl. onboard compute + power; the [ACT](entities/act.md) + co-training-with-static-data reference; ALOHA-2 is in LeRobot's 8 supported platforms (~€21k). (12 sources)
- [Trossen ViperX 300](entities/viperx-300.md) — 6-DOF benchtop arm; the bimanual-teleop SKU underneath [ALOHA / Mobile ALOHA](entities/aloha.md). (3 sources)
- [Grievous](entities/grievous.md) — Alex Koven's WIP cheap-bimanual-mobile testbed; design based on [Mobile ALOHA](entities/aloha.md) + [XLeRobot](entities/xlerobot.md); software on [LeRobot](entities/lerobot.md); RPi5-host + remote-PC. First downstream-of-Mobile-ALOHA project ingested here. (3 sources)
- [Reachy 2](entities/reachy.md) — Pollen Robotics' open-source bimanual mobile manipulator for embodied AI; ROS 2; 7 DOF/arm; natively supported in LeRobot; announced [Jetson Thor](entities/jetson-thor.md) integration for VLA deployment (July 2026). (7 sources)
- [Reachy Mini](entities/reachy-mini.md) — [Pollen](entities/pollen-robotics.md)/[HF](entities/hugging-face.md) desktop HRI robot; **10,000+ shipped** as of Aug 2026. *"AI that interacts"* — speech-first, the counterpart to [Microduck](entities/microduck.md)'s *"AI that acts."* Docs live on the HF Hub. Not yet ingested from its own materials. (2 sources) _stub_
- [myAGV](entities/myagv.md) — Elephant Robotics autonomous mobile base; ROS; Raspberry Pi 4B; pairs with arms. (1 source)
- [LeKiwi](entities/lekiwi.md) — SIGRobotics-UIUC 3-wheel Kiwi-drive holonomic mobile manipulator; Raspberry Pi 5 (or onboard Jetson) + STS3215; **base plate ~216×213 mm**, 4″ omni wheels; sub-$1k; LeRobot ecosystem; 1,300+ stars; ICLR 2026 paper lists at ~€230. (12 sources)
- [XLeRobot](entities/xlerobot.md) — Vector Wang's $660 dual-arm household manipulator (2× SO-ARM101 + LeKiwi base + LeRobot); 90% 3D-printed; v0.3.0 (Aug 2025); 2 winning teams at Oct 2025 Embodied AI Hackathon (incl. matcha-bot champion). (15 sources)
- [SO-ARM101](entities/so-arm101.md) — open-source low-cost arm (The Robot Studio); SO-ARM100 successor; default LeRobot manipulator; SO-10X drives 50%+ of community-contributed LeRobotDatasets as of Sep 2025; **most-tooled platform in the LeRobot↔ROS 2 ecosystem** — reference robot for all 3 bridges (Rosetta `so_101.yaml`, lerobot-ros quickstart, dedicated so101_ros2 workspace); reference platform for NVIDIA's first-party [GR00T](entities/nvidia-groot.md)-1.7 fine-tune walkthrough (2026-07). (23 sources)
- [HopeJR-Arm](entities/hope-jr-arm.md) — humanoid arm + hand from [The Robot Studio](entities/the-robot-studio.md); ~€500; one of 8 LeRobot-supported platforms; BOM in TheRobotStudio/HOPEJr repo. (2 sources)
- [myBuddy 280](entities/mybuddy-280.md) — Elephant Robotics 13 DOF dual-arm desktop robot; $1,619; ROS1. (1 source)
- [ROSOrin](entities/rosorin.md) — Hiwonder's Jetson Orin Nano educational mobile robot kit. (3 sources)
- [ROSOrin Pro](entities/rosorin-pro.md) — Hiwonder's 6-DOF arm + base variant of ROSOrin. (7 sources)
- [ROSOrin Pro 6-DOF arm](entities/rosorin-pro-arm.md) — HX-12H-servo manipulator on the ROSOrin Pro kit. (3 sources)
- [NexArm](entities/nexarm.md) — Hiwonder's LeRobot-native leader-follower imitation-learning arm; 6-DOF, 500mm/500g/±2mm, from $279.99; ACT/Diffusion Policy/π0; dual-camera + drag teaching. A commercial [SO-ARM101](entities/so-arm101.md) competitor. (1 source)
- [FRC KitBot](entities/frc-kitbot.md) — beginner-friendly FRC robot on AndyMark AM14U6 chassis; included in Kickoff Kit. (2 sources)

### Software stacks
- [Perplexity Portable Computer](entities/perplexity-portable-computer.md) — the wiki's **first shipped local-first agent product** (Aug 2026, [DGX Spark](entities/dgx-spark.md) only, Pro/Max subscribers, Linux first). Notable not for the model but for **what else is local**: orchestrator, planner, tool router, scheduler, **durable task queue** and search index. Deterministic orchestrator holds authority and the model only proposes; sandbox **fails closed**; escalation sends only PII-screened, user-approved context to an advisor that **returns text guidance and never touches files or tools**. 85.4% on its own 53-task benchmark with the post-trained PPLX 27B. (3 sources)
- [NeMo Switchyard](entities/nemo-switchyard.md) — NVIDIA's open-source **per-step model router** (accuracy/speed/cost). Claimed to hold frontier task completion at ~⅓ the cost of Opus 4.8 on **unnamed internal benchmarks** — the N-tier generalisation of Perplexity's two-tier advisor, but with no published privacy story and no numbers anyone can check. (1 source)
- [LiteRT](entities/litert.md) — Google's on-device inference runtime, **successor to TensorFlow Lite**, plus **LiteRT-LM** for edge LLMs. **XNNPack** for CPU, **ML Drift** for GPU, NPU delegates; `.litertlm` format with KV-cache management, prompt templating and function calling. The runtime behind every measured on-device LLM figure in this wiki ([Gemma 4](entities/gemma4.md) E2B on Pi 5 / [Orin Nano](entities/jetson-orin-nano.md) / Qualcomm NPU). Notable that it **changes the model**: mixed 2/4/8-bit QAT, embeddings memory-mapped, vision/audio encoders loaded on demand — and a **32K runtime context limit** below the architecture's 128K. (2 sources)
- [NVIDIA OpenShell](entities/nvidia-openshell.md) — the **secure-runtime** layer of NVIDIA's agent stack: containerized sandbox owning isolation, identity, network policy, credential scoping and audit, with the agent's harness started **inside** it rather than invoking it. Resolves a standing wiki question — it is **not** [NeMo Guardrails](entities/nemo-guardrails.md) renamed; NeMo Guardrails is a rail engine in the harness layer, OpenShell is the runtime beneath the security boundary, and by NVIDIA's own criterion only OpenShell's side is authoritative. The closest thing here to the enforcement layer [robot guardrails](syntheses/agents/guardrails-for-robot-agents.md) says ships empty — but its boundary is drawn around processes, files and credentials, the right shape for a robot's *planning* layer and the wrong shape for its *control* layer. Already shipping under [NemoClaw](entities/nemoclaw.md) (`nemohermes onboard` creates the sandbox, then starts [Hermes](entities/hermes-agent.md) in it); **no GA artifact established**. (3 sources)
- [LangChain](entities/langchain.md) — *"the agent engineering platform"* (144 K★, MIT); components + integrations, with [LangGraph](entities/langgraph.md) for orchestration. Reaches this wiki as a **transitive dependency that turns out to be load-bearing** — DimOS calls its `create_agent` directly. Framework churn (chains → agents → platform, 2022–2026) is the real risk for anything pinned to it. (1 source)
- [RTAB-Map](entities/rtab-map.md) — appearance-based RGB-D/LiDAR graph SLAM (IntRoLab, 3,948★); the navigation half of the wiki's only measured onboard-Jetson [XLeRobot](entities/xlerobot.md) build, in **localization-only mode** against a premap. (0 sources) _stub_
- [GTSAM](entities/gtsam.md) — Georgia Tech factor-graph smoothing-and-mapping; the pose-graph-optimization back-end under [DimOS](entities/dimos.md)'s premap+relocalization flow and RTAB-Map-class SLAM. **Factor graphs make loop closure a constraint you re-solve, not a splice.** (0 sources) _stub_
- [CuRobo](entities/curobo.md) — NVIDIA's CUDA-accelerated IK/collision/trajectory library; **the planner under [RoboTwin 2.0](entities/robotwin.md)'s data generator**. Solves IK as *optimization*, so on a 5-DoF arm it returns residual orientation error rather than "infeasible" — the detail that shapes the [5-DoF experiment](syntheses/projects/five-dof-arms-in-robotwin.md). (1 source)
- [LangGraph](entities/langgraph.md) — LangChain's *low-level* stateful-agent orchestration framework (39.6k★, MIT); the agent inside [DimOS](entities/dimos.md)'s `McpClient`, and the reason DimOS breaks the "everyone hand-rolls their dispatcher" convergence. Ships **durable execution + `interrupt()`** — but **verified by code read: DimOS uses the ReAct tool-calling loop only**, no checkpointing, no interrupts, no memory across a crash. The machinery for the wiki's most-cited agentic gap is installed and unused. (2 sources)
- [Drake](entities/drake.md) — MIT CSAIL / **[TRI](entities/tri.md)**-led C++/Python toolbox for **model-based design and verification**; BSD-3, 4,148★, twelve years of daily development. Systems framework + unified math-program solver interface + multibody dynamics with contact. **Not competing with [Isaac Lab](entities/nvidia-isaac-lab.md)** — its value is the *math interface*, not the contact solver, so its real peer set is CasADi/OCS2/Simulink. Ships **LCM** first-class, marks **ROS 2 "unsupported."** The math interface is not abstract: Drake's SPP-in-[GCS](concepts/robotics/graphs-of-convex-sets.md) solver + `IrisInConfigurationSpace` are the whole substrate of the [GCS motion planner](sources/gcs-motion-planning-paper.md). (2 sources)
- [DimOS](entities/dimos.md) — [Dimensional Inc.](entities/dimensional-inc.md)'s Apache-2.0 agentic robotics middleware + agent layer; **3,874★**, ROS-optional (ROS 2 is one of five transports), typed module streams + `autoconnect()` blueprints, `@skill`-by-RPC-introspection served over **MCP**, spatial-memory store, MuJoCo sim + replay, Drake manipulation, hosted WebRTC teleop, and **Quest teleop → [LeRobot](entities/lerobot.md) v3.0 dataset export**. Go2 🟩 / G1 🟨 / xArm 🟨 / Piper 🟨 / drones 🟧. Pre-release beta (`0.0.14b1`); no benchmarks published. (1 source)
- [BehaviorTree.CPP](entities/behaviortree-cpp.md) — MIT C++17 [behavior-tree](concepts/robotics/behavior-trees.md) engine (v4.9); async actions, runtime XML, plugins, type-safe ports/blackboard, Groot2. The execution engine under [Nav2](entities/nav2.md). (2 sources)
- [stretch_ai](entities/stretch-ai.md) — Hello Robot's open-source Python stack with an LLM agent. (8 sources)
- [OpenClaw](entities/openclaw.md) — MIT-licensed local-first personal-AI-assistant framework (TypeScript / Node 24); **375K stars** as of May 2026; multi-platform messaging gateway (20+); "Molty space lobster" naming origin (with unmistakable Claude homophone); ClawHub skill registry. Foundation of the Claw ecosystem. (9 sources)
- [openclaw_controller](entities/openclaw-controller.md) — Hiwonder's ROS 2 bridge module that puts upstream OpenClaw on the [ROSOrin Pro](entities/rosorin-pro.md); exposes the kit's arm / chassis / vision skills (`pick`, `place`, AprilTag pickup, depth grasping, `cmd_vel`, …) as OpenClaw-dispatchable tools. Not a fork of OpenClaw — an extension that sits below it. (5 sources)
- [NemoClaw](entities/nemoclaw.md) — NVIDIA's open-source security/privacy wrapper over [OpenClaw](entities/openclaw.md); bundles NVIDIA Agent Toolkit + OpenShell (policy guardrails) + Nemotron local LLM; targets RTX PCs, RTX Pro, DGX Station, [DGX Spark](entities/dgx-spark.md); `NEMOCLAW_AGENT` selects OpenClaw or [Hermes](entities/hermes-agent.md); early preview. (4 sources)
- [Hermes Agent](entities/hermes-agent.md) — Nous Research's MIT-licensed self-improving agentic framework (Python 89%); **171K stars**; multi-platform messaging (Telegram/Discord/Slack/WhatsApp/Signal/CLI/email); 40+ built-in tools; sub-agents; persistent memory (Honcho dialectic); 200+ models via OpenRouter/Nous Portal/NIM/etc.; MCP integration; `hermes claw migrate` from OpenClaw; runnable as a [NemoClaw](entities/nemoclaw.md) agent variant (`nemohermes`). *"Most used agent in the world according to OpenRouter"* per NVIDIA blog. (7 sources)
- [LeRobot](entities/lerobot.md) — Hugging Face's open-source **end-to-end robot learning library** ([ICLR 2026](sources/lerobot-iclr-2026-paper.md)); 8 platforms; 16K+ datasets / 2.2K+ contributors (Sep 2025); async producer-consumer inference; reference RL + BC + VLA implementations; its `LeRobotDataset` v2 format extended + consumed by the whole [GR00T](entities/nvidia-groot.md) line; **three independent community ROS 2 bridges** (Rosetta / lerobot-ros / so101-ros2); **[GR00T](entities/nvidia-groot.md) 1.7 native since 2026-07** (`policy.type=groot`, Dataset v3.0, `lerobot-rollout` + RTC); July 2026 NVIDIA partnership adds EnvHub/Lab-Arena, Cosmos-3-soon, Thor+Reachy-2. (31 sources)
- [Rosetta](entities/rosetta.md) — solo-author Apache-2.0 bridge from [LeRobot](entities/lerobot.md) to [ROS 2](entities/ros2.md) (76 stars, Sep 2025); YAML-contract-driven topic-to-LeRobot-feature mapping; ships SO-101 + TurtleBot3 reference contracts + experimental HIL support; extends supported policies with π0.5, GR00T, Wall-X, X-VLA. Distro-agnostic; best fit for non-arm or non-Jazzy ROS 2 robots. (7 sources)
- [lerobot-ros](entities/lerobot-ros.md) — generic Python-class-based LeRobot↔ROS 2 wrapper by ycheng517 (**194 stars**, the most popular of 3 bridges; Jul 2025); Jazzy only; ros2_control + MoveIt Servo; minimal 2-package architecture; no license listed; arm-focused. (6 sources)
- [so101-ros2](entities/so101-ros2.md) — SO-101-specific MIT-licensed ROS 2 workspace + LeRobot bridge by nimiCurtis (50 stars, Jun 2025); Humble only; 8 packages incl. URDF/USD + Isaac Sim 5.0+ integration; tested SmolVLA + π0.5 deployment; needs author's LeRobot fork. (5 sources)
- [ros2-mcp-server](entities/ros2-mcp-server.md) — first-party MIT skeleton; the wiki's **first ROS 2↔MCP bridge** (agent↔ROS 2 tool-calling, a layer *above* the LeRobot↔ROS 2 bridges — calls [Rosetta](entities/rosetta.md) underneath). Config-driven tools, structured-result envelope, deterministic dispatch; the fleet framework's Layer-2 bridge; the manipulation-first counterpart to [AgenticROS](entities/agenticros.md), whose capability-flag / mission-graph / heartbeat / fleet-query patterns it adopted 2026-07-05. (4 sources)
- [AgenticROS](entities/agenticros.md) — community Apache-2.0 ROS 2↔agent bridge (TypeScript); OpenClaw native plugin + shared MCP server (Claude/Codex/Hermes) + Gemini CLI + sandboxed NemoClaw; typed capability manifests, mission step-graphs, fleet heartbeat + `find_robots_for` capability queries, 4 transports incl. Zenoh, skills marketplace. Nav-first — no manipulation/LeRobot story; anonymous org, no releases. (2 sources)
- [ROS 2](entities/ros2.md) — Open Robotics' middleware framework; LTS distros Humble (2022, EOL May 2027) + Jazzy (2024, EOL May 2029); operationally load-bearing for LeRobot↔ROS 2 bridge selection. (11 sources)
- [MoveIt](entities/moveit.md) — PickNik-maintained ROS 2 motion-planning + kinematics stack (MoveIt 2); MoveIt Servo for real-time end-effector control; the canonical Cartesian-planning layer on top of ros2_control; used by [lerobot-ros](entities/lerobot-ros.md) (CARTESIAN_VELOCITY mode), [so101-ros2](entities/so101-ros2.md), [myBuddy 280](entities/elephant-robotics.md). (4 sources)
- [Nav2](entities/nav2.md) — canonical ROS 2 autonomous navigation stack (Open Navigation); SLAM + costmaps + behavior tree + planner + controller; successor to ROS 1 `move_base`; the "house navigation is solved" layer bundled with every mobile platform here ([Stretch](entities/stretch.md), [ROSOrin Pro](entities/rosorin-pro.md), [TurtleBot](entities/turtlebot.md)); composes with [LeRobot](entities/lerobot.md) policies (Nav2 = nav, LeRobot = visuomotor manipulation). (5 sources)
- [Matter](entities/matter.md) — the IP-based smart-home interoperability standard ([CSA](entities/connectivity-standards-alliance.md)) beneath Apple Home / Google Home / Alexa / SmartThings. **Multi-admin isolates per-fabric *configuration*, not operational state, and specifies no arbitration at all** — it works because a bulb's state is trivial, which a robot's is not. Its **Access Restriction List** is a shipping vendor-authored bound on what an ecosystem may touch, overriding the ecosystem's own administrator — the consumer-scale precedent for the capability-manifest pattern [AgenticROS](entities/agenticros.md) and [ros2-mcp-server](entities/ros2-mcp-server.md) reached independently. No robot device type. (1 source)
- [PX4 Autopilot](entities/px4-autopilot.md) — Dronecode Foundation's BSD-licensed open-source autopilot for UAVs / drones (multirotor / fixed-wing / VTOL / heli / rover / experimental); NuttX RTOS + uORB + MAVLink + ROS 2 bridge; v1.17.0 stable (2026-05-13); first-class Neural Networks subsystem (TFLM + RAPTOR Adaptive RL + MC NN Control). (5 sources)
- [JetPack SDK](entities/jetpack.md) — NVIDIA's bundled software stack for Jetson products; Jetson Linux + CUDA + cuDNN + TensorRT + DeepStream + VPI + DLA. Current production 6.2.2. (12 sources)
- [Jetson Linux (L4T)](entities/jetson-linux.md) — the L4T BSP underneath JetPack; R36.5 current for Orin (Ubuntu 22.04 + kernel 5.15 + UEFI + OP-TEE). (9 sources)
- [Isaac ROS](entities/isaac-ros.md) — NVIDIA's GPU-accelerated ROS 2 perception/navigation packages; the edge counterpart to Isaac Sim/Lab. **4.x is Thor / x86 / DGX Spark only — Orin was dropped**, terminal at 3.2 on JetPack 6.2. (5 sources)
- [Isaac ROS NVBlox (nvblox)](entities/nvblox.md) — NVIDIA GPU 3D volumetric mapping from RGB-D/stereo depth; Seeed packages it as a `reComputer run nvblox` recipe. (2 sources)
- [NVIDIA DeepStream](entities/nvidia-deepstream.md) — NVIDIA's GStreamer-based **streaming-analytics SDK** (v9.1); multi-stream multi-model inference on Jetson→dGPU; TensorRT + tracking + Triton/TAO/**Metropolis**; the perception plumbing under Metropolis-VSS that [Halos Outside-In](entities/nvidia-halos.md) safety runs on. Classical vision-AI, distinct from [Isaac ROS](entities/isaac-ros.md) robot perception. (1 source)
- [Jetson Device Skills](entities/jetson-device-skills.md) — NVIDIA's catalog of on-device **[agent skills](concepts/agents/agent-skills.md)** (JetPack 7.2); 8 `SKILL.md` bundles teaching Claude Code / Cursor / Codex to inspect + tune a live Jetson (diagnostics, memory-audit, LLM-serve/benchmark, speculative-decoding). Apache-2.0/CC-BY-4.0. (2 sources)

### Controllers / edge AI compute
- [roboRIO](entities/roborio.md) — NI's mandatory FRC robot controller (ARM Cortex-A9 + FPGA); WPILib ecosystem. (2 sources)
- [Jetson AGX Orin](entities/jetson-agx-orin.md) — **top of the Ampere Jetson ladder**; three SKUs (**64 GB / 32 GB / Industrial**) on a 100 × 87 mm **699-pin Molex Mirror Mezz** module. What the tier buys over the [Orin NX](entities/jetson-orin-nx.md) is **memory and IO** — 256-bit LPDDR5 at **204.8 GB/s**, up to 64 GB, 16 CSI lanes, 10 GbE, 2× CAN — for 15–60 W and ~$1,999 (64 GB dev kit). **The smallest module in this wiki that runs a 3B VLA with headroom**, and the only Orin with a measured VLA number: **GR00T N1.6-3B at 173 ms / 5.8 Hz** on TensorRT. Same headline-TOPS caveat as the NX, worse: **46% of the 32 GB's 200 TOPS is DLA**, so its dense-GPU figure (54) is 27% of the box number. 32 GB gains Super Mode (200 → **241 TOPS**) on JetPack 7.2. (7 sources)
- [Jetson Orin NX](entities/jetson-orin-nx.md) — the **middle rung of the Ampere Jetson ladder** and this wiki's default for a battery robot running a VLA onboard: **16 GB / 157 TOPS / 10–40 W / ~$600**, 69.6 × 45 mm 260-pin SO-DIMM, **pin-compatible with the Orin Nano carrier** so it is a literal drop-in. Written 2026-08-28 to close a gap the lint found — "Orin NX" was on 37 pages with its specs scattered and no canonical home. Carries the arithmetic that reframes the headline: **157 = 77 GPU + 80 DLA, both sparse INT8**, so a stock PyTorch/TensorRT-on-GPU VLA sees **38–77**, not 157. Plus three deployment traps with primaries: the **J401 carrier cannot cool Super Mode**, the **10 W mode has an open reboot-crash bug**, and **Isaac ROS 4.x dropped Orin entirely**. (8 sources)
- [Jetson Orin Nano](entities/jetson-orin-nano.md) — NVIDIA's entry-tier Ampere-GPU edge-AI module + Developer Kit; substrate for ROSOrin / ROSOrin Pro / many wiki-tracked educational robots; containerized [LeRobot](entities/lerobot.md) target via [jetson-containers](entities/jetson-containers.md). (24 sources)
- [Jetson Thor](entities/jetson-thor.md) — NVIDIA's Blackwell-generation on-robot compute; family now spans **T5000/T4000 (128/64 GB) + new T3000 (32 GB) / T2000 (16 GB) / IGX T3000** (safety-certified, GA Q1 2027) — "70 TOPS to 2,000 TFLOPS"; 7.5× / 3.5× vs Orin; **no RT cores → can't host Isaac Sim**; measured GR00T N1.6 inference **10.9 Hz official TRT / 22–24 Hz community**. (31 sources)
- [NVIDIA DGX Spark](entities/dgx-spark.md) — GB10 Grace Blackwell desktop AI supercomputer; 128 GB unified memory, 4th-gen RT cores, ConnectX-7; train-on-Spark / deploy-on-Thor split; the **local-AI-server** tier for on-robot agents (runs Gemma-4-31B / a 120B Hermes brain); CUDA-13 torch pin for LeRobot/GR00T fine-tuning. (8 sources)
- [NVIDIA Halos](entities/nvidia-halos.md) — NVIDIA's **full-stack functional-safety system** for physical AI (silicon→OS→middleware→apps) on the **IGX [Thor](entities/jetson-thor.md)** SoM (Functional Safety Island; Halos OS = Linux+QNX); Inside-Out ([Digit](entities/digit.md)) vs Outside-In safety (open-source [blueprint](sources/halos-outside-in-safety-github.md)); first **ANAB-accredited** AI-functional-safety inspection program (TÜV Rheinland). The certified deterministic layer under a learned VLA. (4 sources)
- [stable-worldmodel](entities/stable-worldmodel.md) — Python infrastructure under LeWorldModel (env zoo + planning API + dataset format). DM Control + Gymnasium-Robotics Fetch + classic + OGBench + more. Now also a **generalization benchmark** with controllable visual/physical factors of variation + a Lance data layer; its [May 2026 paper](sources/stable-worldmodel-paper.md) is the source of the world-model brittleness result. CC BY 4.0. (3 sources)
- [Pixhawk](entities/pixhawk.md) — open-hardware flight-controller standard (Dronecode); FMUv3–v6X-RT family; 30+ manufacturer-supported boards (Holybro, CUAV, CubePilot, ARK, ModalAI); the dominant hardware target for PX4. (2 sources)

### Motor SDKs / hardware components
- [Damiao (DM motors)](entities/damiao.md) — Chinese **quasi-direct-drive CAN actuators** (DM-J4310-2EC, DM-J4340P-2EC); the motors in the [reBot Arm B601-DM](entities/rebot-arm-b601.md). A **third motor tier** above FeeTech/Dynamixel serial servos: 1 Mbit/s CAN with an MIT-style impedance interface (pos + vel + kp/kd + feedforward torque) instead of position-only commands. Motor IDs set from a **Windows-only** GUI. No torque/gear/thermal specs published; backdrivability unmeasured. (2 sources)
- [Robstride (RS motors)](entities/robstride.md) — the sibling CAN-actuator lineage (ROBSTRIDE 00 / 06); the motors in the [reBot Arm B601-RS](entities/rebot-arm-b601.md). Runs at **48 V** and reaches 2.5 kg payload / <0.1 mm repeatability versus the DM build's 24 V / 1.5 kg / 0.2 mm. Integrates via standard **SocketCAN**, the cleaner of the two paths. No primary datasheet ingested. (1 source)
- [OpenFT sensor](entities/openft-sensor.md) — [Dimensional](entities/dimensional-inc.md)'s open **6-axis F/T sensor** via Hall-effect magnetic displacement; Gerbers + JLCPCB BOM + drivers. Aimed at the affordable tier's **position-only recording** limit. Published-not-maintained: 2★, no license, **no specs at all**. (1 source)
- [FeeTech](entities/feetech.md) — Chinese smart serial bus servos (STS3215, **STS3250**, SCS series); the low-cost / hobby-tier motor lineage; one of two SDKs LeRobot's middleware natively integrates ([ICLR 2026 paper §3.1](sources/lerobot-iclr-2026-paper.md)); used by SO-100/101, LeKiwi, Stretch 4 tool bus, and [Sourccey](entities/sourccey.md) (12 servos on one 1 Mbaud UART bus; **limited backdrivability** documented). (6 sources)
- [Dynamixel](entities/dynamixel.md) — ROBOTIS (Korea) smart serial bus servos (XL430, XM430, XM540); the research / education premium-tier motor lineage; one of two SDKs LeRobot's middleware natively integrates; used by Koch-v1.1, LeKiwi alt-config. (5 sources)
- [Orbbec (Gemini2)](entities/orbbec.md) — RGB-D depth-camera vendor; the Gemini2 is the depth source in Seeed's [nvblox](entities/nvblox.md) mapping recipe; a RealSense alternative on Jetson. (2 sources)

### Formats / standards
- [Zenoh](entities/zenoh.md) — Eclipse/ZettaScale pub/sub **+ storage + queryables** protocol; 3,078★, EPL-2.0/Apache-2.0. Simultaneously **a [ROS 2](entities/ros2.md) backend** (`rmw_zenoh`) *and* **a way to not use ROS 2** ([DimOS](entities/dimos.md)'s reliable transport) — the middleware stack has come apart into separable discovery/transport/API layers. **Now measured**: Zenoh P2P **16 µs vs Cyclone DDS 37 µs** across a real network; 5-byte wire overhead; 300 bytes on an 8-bit micro. (2 sources)
- [OpenUSD](entities/openusd.md) — open scene-description + robotics physics-schema layer (UsdPhysics, MjcPhysics, NewtonSceneAPI). (6 sources)
- [MAVLink](entities/mavlink.md) — lightweight binary telemetry / command protocol for drones (Dronecode); spoken by PX4 + ArduPilot + QGroundControl + MAVSDK + MAVROS. (2 sources)

### Datasets
- [Index (Figure AI)](entities/figure-index.md) — **amended 2026-08-28**: Figure's one published human→robot transfer result, [Go-Big](sources/figure-project-go-big.md), covers **navigation only**, so the gap this corpus is meant to cross remains unaddressed for manipulation. [Figure](entities/figure.md)'s crowdsourced corpus of **human** egocentric task video, collected by a consumer app paying the public per clip (Aug 2026). Claimed **43,200 h/day ingest**, 16M+ videos, $15M paid out — but **total hours are never stated**, diversity is quoted as a size-invariant rate (373 tasks / 1,146 objects / 116 environments per 1,000 h), and the accepted fraction behind five filtering stages is undisclosed. Contains **no robot data and no action labels**. Figure-exclusive, unreleased, no results published. (1 source)
- [RoboMIND](entities/robomind.md) — 107k real teleop trajectories / 305.5 h / 479 tasks / 4 embodiments on **one standardized platform**; **19.9% of [X-VLA](entities/x-vla.md)'s pretraining**. The only dataset here with **5k annotated failure trajectories** and an [Isaac Sim](entities/nvidia-isaac-sim.md) digital twin — both unused downstream. Its 15,187 dexterous-hand humanoid trajectories are excluded by X-VLA's binary-gripper action space. All baselines n=10. (2 sources)
- [Dolma](entities/dolma.md) — Ai2's **open LLM pretraining corpus**: 3T tokens / >4B docs from 6 sources, curated from ~200 TB, with an open curation toolkit; the data half of [OLMo](entities/olmo.md)'s "truly open" (ODC-BY). (3 sources)
- [DROID](entities/droid.md) — Distributed Robot Interaction Dataset; 350 hr / 76k traj / 564 scenes of Franka Panda teleop; the dominant real-robot dataset in JEPA-for-robotics work; [MolmoAct2-DROID](entities/molmoact2.md) is a 74.6k-episode quality-filtered subset; also the finetuning set for **Cosmos3-Edge-Policy-DROID**. (21 sources)
- [Open X-Embodiment (OXE)](entities/open-x-embodiment.md) — 22-embodiment + ~500-skill umbrella corpus (O'Neill et al. 2024); the standard cross-embodiment pretraining corpus for [π0](entities/pi-zero.md), [Octo](entities/octo.md), [OpenVLA](entities/openvla.md), [GR00T N1](sources/groot-n1-paper.md). [DROID](entities/droid.md) is the Franka-Panda subset; ~1,150 h in the [TRI LBM](sources/tri-lbm-paper.md) pretraining mix. (12 sources)
- [EgoDex](entities/egodex.md) — 829 hr Apple Vision Pro–captured egocentric dataset; 194 tabletop manipulation tasks; clean wrist + hand keypoints. The high-precision complement to in-the-wild egocentric data in [EgoScale](sources/egoscale-paper.md) pretraining. (2 sources)

### Model organisms / connectomes
- [Drosophila melanogaster](entities/drosophila.md) — fruit fly; canonical "whole-organism AI" target; substrate for both [FlyWire](entities/flywire.md) and [flybody](entities/flybody.md). (6 sources)
- [FlyWire](entities/flywire.md) — international consortium + dataset for the complete adult *Drosophila* brain connectome (139,255 neurons, ~50M synapses; *Nature* 2024). (5 sources)
- [H01](entities/h01-connectome.md) — Lichtman Lab / Google **human-cortex** connectome fragment: 1.4 PB, ~1 mm³, 183M synapses, 100 proofread cells. A dense sample, not a complete wiring diagram. (1 source)
- [Drosophila brain model](entities/drosophila-brain-model.md) — Phil Shiu's MIT-licensed Brian 2 LIF model on the FlyWire connectome (paper code). (3 sources)
- [flyvis](entities/flyvis.md) — TuragaLab's MIT-licensed PyTorch connectome-constrained DMN of the fly visual system; v1.1.3 March 2026. (2 sources)

### Biohybrid / living machines
- [Xenobots (reconfigurable organisms)](entities/xenobots.md) — AI-designed living machines built from frog (*Xenopus*) stem cells; two generations (sculpted-cardiac 2020 → self-assembling cilia-driven 2021); locomotion + manipulation + collective behavior + self-repair + spontaneous kinematic self-replication. Biological counterpart to code-based self-replication. (5 sources)

### Programming games / digital ALife
- [Conway's Game of Life](entities/game-of-life.md) — John Conway's 1970 2D [cellular automaton](concepts/alife/cellular-automata.md); one neighbor-count rule → gliders, glider guns, Turing-completeness. The archetypal Class-4 CA; subject of [Wolfram's construction-vs-search innovation study](sources/wolfram-2025-game-of-life-engineering.md). (1 source)
- [Core War](entities/core-war.md) — 1984 programming game (Dewdney & Jones); Redcode "warriors" battle in a circular memory via the MARS VM; the **Imp** is a one-instruction self-replicator. The self-replicating programming-game ancestor of the wiki's [Computational Life](sources/computational-life-self-replicating-programs-paper.md) thread. (6 sources)
- [Tierra](entities/tierra.md) — Thomas Ray's 1991 digital-evolution system; a hand-written self-replicating ancestor evolves a whole ecology (parasites/hyper-parasites/sociality/cheaters) in a memory "soup." Open-ended evolution. (2 sources)
- [Avida](entities/avida.md) — Adami/Brown 1994 tierra-inspired digital-evolution platform; 2D + local interactions; rewards computational tasks so complexity evolves. The standard digital-evolution research tool; substrate for the famous Lenski et al. 2003 EQU result. (2 sources)

### Vision foundation models
- [DINOv2](entities/dinov2.md) — Meta FAIR self-supervised ViT (142M images, ViT-S/B/L/g); substrate for DINO-WM, DINO-world, JEPA-WMs. Apache 2.0. (10 sources)
- [DINOv3](entities/dinov3.md) — Meta AI Research 7B-parameter ViT SSL foundation model (Aug 2025); Gram anchoring fixes dense-feature degradation; new SSL state-of-the-art on dense tasks (COCO mAP 66.1 frozen, ADE20k mIoU 63.0). **First downstream use in the wiki: [TurboVLA](entities/turbovla.md)'s entire vision pathway.** (7 sources)
- [Grounding DINO](entities/grounding-dino.md) — open-set detector whose **bidirectional cross-modal feature enhancer** [TurboVLA](entities/turbovla.md) reuses (and initializes from) as a robot policy's vision-language fusion. Raises the live question: which kind of vision-language pretraining does manipulation actually need — *grounding* or *generative language*? (1 source)

### Perception / tracking models
- [TrackNet (model family)](entities/tracknet.md) — heatmap-based tracker for **tiny fast objects** (tennis balls, shuttlecocks) in video. Four versions across **four different groups** (NCTU V1/V2 → ACM MM Asia V3 → ANU V4), not one lab's roadmap. Beats YOLOv7 by ~30 F1 at this object scale; assumes a **static camera**; **degrades on cross-venue transfer** — observed first-party on pinball in 2026-07 (0.914 → 0.555 across two unseen machines), closer to V1's cross-*court* recall drop than its cross-*sport* collapse. Two implementations, split MIT/GPL and PyTorch/Keras. (8 sources)

- [WebSSL / Web-DINO](entities/webssl.md) — FAIR/NYU language-free SSL family, **1B–7B params on 2B MetaCLIP images** ([primary](sources/webssl-paper.md)). **The wiki was carrying opposite verdicts on it under two names**: as *WebSSL* it is [Patch Policy](entities/patch-policy.md)'s co-winning frozen backbone for robot learning; as *Web-DINO* it scores **0.16 action R²** with negative rotation, clustered with the worst encoders. Both true — **a strong perception front-end and a weak dynamics substrate**, and it is trained on still images. **The encoder recommended for policies is one of the worst measured choices for a latent world model.** (4 sources) Filed because it is **the best-performing frozen backbone for robot policy learning** in the only head-to-head the wiki holds ([Patch Policy](entities/patch-policy.md)'s five-encoder sweep), co-recommended with [DINOv2](entities/dinov2.md). Thin by necessity — the WebSSL primary is unfiled, so architecture, scale and objective are undocumented. **Never tested as a world-model encoder**, so whether it shares image-SSL's rotation collapse is unknown. (1 source)

### VLM backbones (for VLAs)
- [Moondream](entities/moondream.md) — M87 Labs' Apache-2.0 **tiny VLM** (3.1 = 9 B MoE / 2 B active; the 2 B dense is what actually gets run, 2.39 M HF downloads). **Query/Caption/Detect/Point/Segment**; **pointing** puts it beside [Molmo](entities/molmo.md). [DimOS](entities/dimos.md)'s local VLM option. Publishes the wiki's only small-VLM **edge latency ladder** — Thor 246 ms, AGX Orin 514 ms — vendor-measured, Photon-dependent. (1 source)
- [Florence-2](entities/florence-2.md) — Microsoft's 2024 unified VLM (Base 0.23 B / Large 0.77 B); the encoder inside [X-VLA](entities/x-vla.md), used **asymmetrically** (main view + language through the VLM, wrist views bypass it to a shared ViT). The wiki's strongest evidence that **backbone choice is not where cross-embodiment VLA performance comes from**. (1 source)
- [PaLI-X](entities/pali-x.md) — Google's 55B ViT + encoder-decoder VLM; backbone of [RT-2](entities/rt-2.md) and [RT-H](entities/rt-h.md). ~275× [TurboVLA](entities/turbovla.md)'s total parameter count, which is the scale confound in RT-H's language-beats-labels result. (1 source)
- [Eagle (NVIDIA VLM family)](entities/eagle-vlm.md) — NVIDIA's VLM line; **VLM backbone of [GR00T](entities/nvidia-groot.md) through N1.5** (Eagle-2 → Eagle 2.5). Eagle-1 = mixture-of-encoders/channel-concat (ICLR 2025); Eagle 2.5 = single SigLIP-so400M + long-context. Superseded by [Cosmos](entities/nvidia-cosmos.md) from N1.6. (5 sources)
- [SigLIP 2](entities/siglip-2.md) — filed **separately from [SigLIP](entities/siglip.md)** because every control measurement the wiki holds names the 2 variant specifically and should not be back-propagated. Inside [GR00T N1](entities/nvidia-groot.md)'s Eagle-2 VLM and [Molmo2-ER](entities/molmo2-er.md). The evidence **does not point one way**: weak as an action-decoding substrate (**0.17** R², negative on rotation) and weakest of five frozen policy backbones — but **best generated-latent success-classifier accuracy** as a diffusion-world-model latent space. Poor at *decoding actions*, decent at *judging whether a rollout succeeded*; only the first is what a planner needs. (5 sources)
- [SigLIP](entities/siglip.md) — Google's sigmoid-loss image–text encoder; the **default semantic vision encoder of the 2025–26 VLA generation** ([PaliGemma](entities/paligemma.md)→[π0](entities/pi-zero.md), [SmolVLM](entities/smolvlm.md)→[SmolVLA](entities/smolvla.md), single SigLIP-so400M in [Eagle 2.5](entities/eagle-vlm.md)→[GR00T N1.5](entities/nvidia-groot.md), DINOv2+SigLIP in [OpenVLA-OFT](entities/openvla.md)). **Two independent 2026 results say its features are poor for control**: 0.17 action R² after inverse-dynamics tuning vs V-JEPA 2's 0.85, **negative on rotation**, and "the limitation is representational rather than optimization-related"; and it is the weakest of five frozen policy backbones. The synthesis: **an excellent semantic encoder and a poor geometric one** — which is why VLAs that care about contact pair it with DINOv2 rather than using it alone. No primary ingested. (3 sources)
- [PaliGemma](entities/paligemma.md) — Google's 3 B VLM (SigLIP + Gemma 2B; Beyer et al. 2024); backbone of [π0](entities/pi-zero.md). (2 sources) _stub_
- [Gemma3](entities/gemma3.md) — Google's 2025 VLM family (1B/4B/12B/27B; built-in 400M vision encoder); backbone of [π0.7](entities/pi07.md). (1 source)
- [Gemma 4](entities/gemma4.md) — Google's 2026 multimodal family; **first MoE Gemma**; edge variants (E2B/E4B multimodal) → Jetson, 31B → DGX Spark; native function-calling; an on-robot System-2 candidate. (3 sources)
- [SmolVLM-2](entities/smolvlm.md) — Hugging Face's compact ~0.4 B VLM (SigLIP + SmolLM2; Marafioti et al. 2025); backbone of [SmolVLA](entities/smolvla.md). (0 sources)
- [Molmo](entities/molmo.md) — Allen Institute (Ai2) **fully-open** (weights + PixMo data) VLM family (Deitke et al. 2409.17146); signature **pointing** capability; distillation-free **PixMo** data recipe; Molmo-72B tops academics / 2nd human-pref behind GPT-4o; backbone of [MolmoAct](entities/molmoact.md). **Molmo2** → [Molmo2-ER](entities/molmo2-er.md) is MolmoAct2's backbone. (5 sources)
- [Molmo2-ER](entities/molmo2-er.md) — Ai2's embodied-reasoning VLM (a Molmo2-4B fine-tune on a 3.3M-sample spatial corpus, specialize-then-rehearse); **63.8% avg on 13 embodied-reasoning benchmarks, beating GPT-5 and Gemini-ER 1.5**; the [MolmoAct2](entities/molmoact2.md) backbone. (4 sources)
- [OLMo](entities/olmo.md) — Ai2's **truly-open** LLM family (weights + **[Dolma](entities/dolma.md)** data + code + checkpoints + logs, Apache 2.0); OLMo 1 (1B/7B) → OLMo 2 (7B/13B/32B); the open **decoder** at the base of [Molmo](entities/molmo.md)'s 100%-open stack. (5 sources)
- [OLMoE](entities/olmoe.md) — Ai2's fully-open **[Mixture-of-Experts](concepts/learning/mixture-of-experts.md)** LLM (7B total / 1B active, 64×top-8); backbone of Molmo's MolmoE-1B; the wiki's open MoE reference. (3 sources)

### Generative models for image / world
- [Marble](entities/marble.md) — [World Labs](entities/world-labs.md)' world-generation model: text / image / video / **spatial-sketch** prompts → explorable 3D environments, emitting **Gaussian splats for viewing and collision meshes a physics engine can operate on, from one model**. The company's claimed proof that the renderer/simulator boundary dissolves. **No architecture, parameters, or benchmark anywhere** — and both category caveats apply: splats optimized for plausibility over truth, generated geometry that "can look correct while containing self-intersections or wrong scale." (2 sources)
- [BAGEL](entities/bagel.md) — 14B mixture-of-transformers image-gen + editing model (2025); used as the subgoal-image world model substrate in [π0.7](entities/pi07.md). (0 sources)

### Generative models
- [DDPM](entities/ddpm.md) — Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Models (Ho, Jain, Abbeel; NeurIPS 2020); foundational diffusion-model class; substrate of [Diffusion Policy](entities/diffusion-policy.md), [NVIDIA Cosmos](entities/nvidia-cosmos.md), [Genie Envisioner](entities/genie-envisioner.md). (8 sources)
- [GraspGen / GraspGen-X](entities/graspgen.md) — [NVIDIA](entities/nvidia.md)'s 6-DOF grasp generator: SE(3) **diffusion** sampler + discriminator ranked in a flywheel. **GraspGen-X** (CVPR 2026) conditions both halves on a **12-dim swept-volume** description of the gripper, so one model serves grippers it has never seen — **0.506** mAUC zero-shot on 10 novel real grippers, **79.0%** on a real UR10 with a Robotiq-2F140. Trained on 350 M ACRONYM/[Isaac Sim](entities/nvidia-isaac-sim.md) grasps from **procedurally generated** grippers (2 B in the released checkpoint). Post-CVPR release adds a **Grasp Mixture-of-Experts** — a PCA oriented-bounding-box sampler alongside the diffusion one, because users needed **enforced top-down grasps**. Model/code/dataset promised on NVlabs. (1 source)

### World models
- [NVIDIA Cosmos](entities/nvidia-cosmos.md) — world foundation model + simulation engine (generative video); **Cosmos 3** (June 2026) unified it into an omnimodal Mixture-of-Transformers [world-action model](concepts/world-models/world-action-model.md); also the VLM backbone of [GR00T](entities/nvidia-groot.md) N1.6 (Cosmos-2B) + N1.7 (Cosmos-Reason2-2B); coming to [LeRobot](entities/lerobot.md) "soon" (July 2026). **Cosmos 3 Edge (4B) shipped 2026-07-20** — 15 Hz on [Jetson Thor](entities/jetson-thor.md), a DROID policy variant, and step-distilled 64B Super models. (26 sources)
- [DreamGen](entities/dreamgen.md) — NVIDIA GEAR; video-WM-as-**synthetic-data-generator** ("neural trajectories"); root of the Dream\* line ([DreamGen](sources/dreamgen-paper.md) → DreamZero → [DreamDojo](sources/dreamdojo-paper.md)); the synthetic layer of the [GR00T](entities/nvidia-groot.md) data pyramid. (2 sources)
- [Genie Envisioner](entities/genie-envisioner.md) — AGIBOT's world simulator GE-Sim2 (generative video). (8 sources)
- [Genie 3](entities/genie-3.md) — Google DeepMind's general-purpose generative world model (photorealistic interactive 3D environments); foundation the [Waymo World Model](sources/waymo-world-model.md) is post-trained from. Distinct from AGIBOT's Genie Envisioner/Sim. Coherent for only **a few minutes** at its 2025 release before objects shift or vanish (per the [HAI brief](sources/hai-world-model-spatial-intelligence-brief.md)) — now **measured**: 1st of 10 in [WorldRoamBench](entities/worldroambench.md) FPV (73.81), winning on memory while 7th on action following. **No Genie 3 paper exists** — params, architecture, and training data are all undisclosed. (3 sources)
- [Waymo World Model](entities/waymo.md) — Waymo's Genie-3-derived generative-video WM for AV simulation; camera+lidar output; three control axes (action/scene/language). (1 source)
- [V-JEPA 2](entities/v-jepa-2.md) — Meta FAIR's JEPA world model (latent prediction); zero-shot Franka. (21 sources)
- [LeWorldModel](entities/leworldmodel.md) — first stable end-to-end JEPA from raw pixels. (28 sources)
- [LpWM (LpWorldModel)](entities/lpwm.md) — [LeWM](entities/leworldmodel.md)'s encoder and CEM planner with **RDMReg** sparse non-negative latents instead of SIGReg's dense Gaussian. Sparsity **lowers the predictor capacity needed to plan** (+24–57% on PushT at intermediate capacity; nothing at high capacity); codes are **mode-factored** (support = discrete regime, 94–99% decodable; magnitude = within-regime state). (1 source)
- [AdaJEPA](entities/adajepa.md) — latent world model that **adapts at test time inside the MPC loop**, one gradient step per replan, using the observed transition as a free label. Nearly doubles planning success on unseen shapes; no harm in-distribution; modest under recoloring, because that destroys the identity signal rather than miscalibrating it. (1 source)
- [WorldDP](entities/worlddp.md) — LeCun-coauthored (NYU + AMI Labs) hierarchical planner: object-centric JEPA world model proposes subgoals, a [Diffusion Policy](entities/diffusion-policy.md) executes; SOTA on multi-stage OGBench manipulation. (1 source)
- [SMWM (Sensorimotor World Model)](entities/smwm.md) — Balestriero + Schölkopf JEPA world model using inverse-dynamics regularization as the sole anti-collapse mechanism; recovers controllable DoF, filters distractors. (1 source)
- [LeNEPA](entities/lenepa.md) — the "Le-" family (LeJEPA/LeWM SIGReg) extended to **time-series** SSL; no-augmentation next-latent prediction. Not a world model — filed here beside its Le-family siblings. (1 source)
- [JEPA-WMs](entities/jepa-wms.md) — FAIR (Terver et al.); first JEPA-for-robotics paper using RoboCasa. (7 sources)
- [DINO-WM](entities/dino-wm.md) — NYU + FAIR; frozen DINOv2 features + learned predictor; zero-shot planning. (22 sources)
- [DINO-world](entities/dino-world.md) — FAIR DINOv2 video world model ("Back to the Features"). (4 sources)
- [VLA-JEPA](entities/vla-jepa.md) — JEPA-as-auxiliary inside a VLA policy (USTC, Sun et al.). (2 sources)
- [VL-JEPA](entities/vl-jepa.md) — JEPA reframing of a full vision-language model (Meta/LeCun, Chen et al.); predicts text embeddings, not tokens. **Distinct from VLA-JEPA despite the name.** (2 sources)
- [HWM (Hierarchical World Models)](entities/hwm.md) — model-agnostic two-level latent-MPC planning wrapper; the realized Hierarchical JEPA (Zhang et al. incl. LeCun). Contrast [WorldDP](entities/worlddp.md) (diffusion-policy low level vs HWM's world-model low level). (3 sources)
- [Dreamer / DreamerV3](entities/dreamer.md) — Hafner-line MBRL family with generative WM + actor-critic in imagination; LeWM baseline; lineage now fully sourced ([PlaNet](sources/planet-paper.md) → [DayDreamer](sources/daydreamer-paper.md) → V3); 2025–26 downstream: [S5WM](sources/s5wm-paper.md) (RSSM→SSM, 4× faster) + [EAWM](sources/eawm-paper.md) (event objective, ICLR-2026 SOTA). (14 sources)
- [TD-MPC / TD-MPC2](entities/td-mpc.md) — Hansen-line decoder-free MBRL with MPC + TD-bootstrapped value; LeWM baseline; closest MBRL relative to JEPA; **the only model-based method natively in LeRobot**. Both papers now filed ([TD-MPC](sources/td-mpc-paper.md) 2022 + [TD-MPC2](sources/td-mpc2-paper.md) 2024). (9 sources)
- [PLDM (Planning with Latent Dynamics Models)](entities/pldm.md) — Sobal-line end-to-end JEPA WM (NYU + FAIR); VICReg + inverse-dynamics + similarity multi-term loss; the canonical "end-to-end JEPA before LeWM" baseline. (2 sources — 2022 precursor + 2025 paper)

### VLA models / generalist policies
- [Helix (Figure AI)](entities/helix.md) — [Figure](entities/figure.md)'s closed VLA, and the wiki's only **three-tier** one. Helix 1 (Feb 2025): S2 7B VLM @ 7–9 Hz + S1 80M transformer @ 200 Hz, upper body, ~500 h teleop. Helix 02 (Jan 2026): adds **System 0** — 10M params @ **1 kHz**, 1,000+ h retargeted human motion, 200k parallel sim envs — which Figure says **"replaces 109,504 lines of hand-engineered C++"**, and rewires S1 to **"all sensors in, all actuators out"** across the whole body. Demonstrated on a **4-minute, 61-action** dishwasher task; later **perception-conditioned** for zero-shot real stairs. **Zero published success rates across both generations** — cite it for architecture, never for performance. (4 sources)
- [LingBot-Map](entities/lingbot-map.md) — [Robbyant](entities/robbyant.md)'s **feed-forward 3D foundation model** for streaming reconstruction; 16,471★, Apache-2.0. Geometric Context Transformer with anchor context / pose-reference window / **trajectory memory**, ~20 FPS at 518×378 over 10,000+ frames via **paged KV cache**. The learned answer to what [RTAB-Map](entities/rtab-map.md) solves by optimization — but no edge latency published and no prior-map relocalization described. (1 source)
- [DP3 (3D Diffusion Policy)](entities/dp3.md) — point-cloud [Diffusion Policy](entities/diffusion-policy.md) variant; **beats every VLA on RoboTwin Easy (55.2) then collapses to 5.0 under randomization (−50.2)**. The benchmark's own authors concede the clean number *"partly stems from perfect point clouds and clean background segmentation in simulation"* — the wiki's clearest case of a sim artifact flattering a policy class. (1 source)
- [The LIBERO tie cluster](entities/libero-tie-models.md) — holding page for **CogVLA 97.4, VLA-Adapter 97.3, MemoryVLA, FLOWER, BAKU**: five models the wiki quotes numbers from and cannot characterize. MemoryVLA held the 7 B Simpler-WidowX SOTA (71.9) that [X-VLA](entities/x-vla.md) beat at 0.9 B; FLOWER holds CALVIN 4.53, **the one benchmark X-VLA lost**. All secondhand. (2 sources)
- [RDT-1B](entities/rdt.md) — Tsinghua's **1.2 B diffusion foundation model for bimanual manipulation** (ICLR 2025); a "Physically Interpretable Unified Action Space" — a **third** answer to cross-embodiment heterogeneity alongside soft prompts and standardized collection. Cited as a baseline in [RoboTwin 2.0](entities/robotwin.md) / [RoboMIND](entities/robomind.md) / [X-VLA](entities/x-vla.md) and never described until now; **smallest Easy→Hard drop of any policy in RoboTwin's table (−20.8)**. Primary paper un-ingested. (4 sources)
- [X-VLA](entities/x-vla.md) — AIR Tsinghua + Shanghai AI Lab; **0.9 B, SOTA on five of six benchmarks**, built on [soft prompts](concepts/learning/soft-prompt-cross-embodiment.md) at 0.04% of params. Simpler-WidowX 95.8 (+23.9 over a 7 B model), LIBERO 98.1, RoboTwin-2.0 hard 39.0 (2.4× prior best), NAVSIM 87.3. **9 M-param LoRA ≈ fully-finetuned π0.** Upstream in [LeRobot](entities/lerobot.md) as `xvla`; **first research VLA shipped preinstalled on a consumer robot** ([Sourccey](entities/sourccey.md)). (3 sources)
- [RT-1](entities/rt-1.md) — the 35M/3 Hz ancestor of the VLA lineage (Google + [Everyday Robots](entities/everyday-robots.md), 2022); 130k demos, 700+ tasks, seen 97 / unseen 76; Kuka data lifts new tasks 22→39. (1 source)
- [UniT](entities/unit.md) — [XPENG](entities/xpeng-robotics.md) visually-anchored **latent action tokenizer**; shared RQ-VAE codebook across human and humanoid; +18.9 pp from the objective alone, ~10× data efficiency, 0→60% zero-shot from human video. The readable-language counter-proposal. (1 source)
- [RT-H](entities/rt-h.md) — [DeepMind](entities/google-deepmind.md)/Stanford **action-hierarchy** policy: language motions as an intermediate layer between task and action, on a [PaLI-X](entities/pali-x.md) 55B backbone. The wiki's primary reference for a **human-readable intermediate action representation**; its OneHot ablation is the strongest evidence that the *words* matter, not just the partition. (1 source)
- [RT-2](entities/rt-2.md) — DeepMind's 2023 VLA; actions as discrete tokens in the VLM's own vocabulary, PaLI-X 55B, 256 bins/dim. The founding discrete-action-token model and ancestor of [OpenVLA](entities/openvla.md). **Documented secondhand via RT-H — the RT-2 and RT-1 papers remain un-ingested.** (1 source)
- [TurboVLA](entities/turbovla.md) — [HUST](entities/hust.md)/[Huawei](entities/huawei.md); the **LLM-free VLA**. 0.2 B params, **0.9 GB inference VRAM, 32 Hz on a consumer RTX 4090**; [LIBERO](entities/libero.md) 97.7 (tied), [RoboTwin 2.0](entities/robotwin.md) 60.2 > π0.5 57.0 (separates). First VLA footprint here that fits an [Orin Nano](entities/jetson-orin-nano.md) 8 GB — though **edge latency is unmeasured**. (1 source)
- [Evo-1](entities/evo-1.md) — 0.8 B lightweight VLA (CVPR 2026); LIBERO 94.8 at **137.2 ms** — the reference case that **small ≠ fast** when a multimodal backbone stays in the loop. (1 source) _stub_
- [UniVLA](entities/univla.md) — 7.6 B generalist VLA built on task-centric **latent actions** (RSS 2025); LIBERO 95.2 at 15.0 GB / 173.8 ms. Worth a primary ingest for the cross-embodiment thread. (1 source) _stub_
- [π0.5](entities/pi-zero-5.md) — [PI](entities/physical-intelligence.md)'s open-world co-training VLA and **the standing 2026 baseline**: cleans unseen homes ([paper](sources/pi-zero-5-paper.md)), yet ~0.00 under instruction paraphrase ([LIBERO-PRO](sources/libero-pro-paper.md)). Scene generalization without instruction generalization. (8 sources)
- [NVIDIA GR00T](entities/nvidia-groot.md) — open VLA bundled with Isaac Lab; **full version line now documented** — N1 ([paper](sources/groot-n1-paper.md); dual-system, data pyramid, 76.8% real GR-1) → [N1.5](sources/groot-n1_5.md) (frozen VLM + FLARE) → [N1.6](sources/groot-n1_6.md) (Cosmos-2B) → **N1.7 GA-in-[LeRobot](entities/lerobot.md) 2026-07** (Cosmos-Reason2-2B + [EgoScale](sources/egoscale-paper.md); [LIBERO](entities/libero.md) avg 96.5%; N1.5 dropped); backbone progression Eagle→Cosmos; [Isaac-GR00T repo](sources/isaac-gr00t-github.md) is the codebase; measured edge inference: [Thor](entities/jetson-thor.md) 10.9 Hz TRT / AGX Orin 5.8 Hz. (39 sources)
- [GEAR-SONIC](entities/gear-sonic.md) — NVIDIA GEAR's humanoid **whole-body controller** (the `UNITREE_G1_SONIC` controller); motion-tracking-as-scaling-task RL on [Unitree G1](entities/unitree-g1.md); FSQ universal-token interface a GR00T N1.5 VLA drives for loco-manipulation; code in [GR00T-WholeBodyControl](sources/gr00t-wholebodycontrol-github.md). (5 sources)
- [π0](entities/pi-zero.md) — [Physical Intelligence](entities/physical-intelligence.md)'s 3.3 B VLA (Oct 2024); PaliGemma 3 B VLM + flow-matching action expert; cross-embodiment teleop on 7 robots / 68 tasks / 10k hours. The canonical flow-matching VLA; LeRobot-supported (lerobot/pi0). (23 sources)
- [π0.7](entities/pi07.md) — [Physical Intelligence](entities/physical-intelligence.md)'s 5 B VLA (2025); Gemma3 4B + MEM video encoder + 860 M flow-matching action expert; diversified prompt with subgoal images + episode metadata; first VLA with emergent compositional generalization (espresso machine, sweet-potato-into-air-fryer out-of-the-box). (7 sources)
- [π*0.6](entities/pistar06.md) — [Physical Intelligence](entities/physical-intelligence.md)'s RL-adapted π0.6 (2025); RECAP recipe = offline-RL pretraining + iterative deployment with advantage-conditioned policy extraction; 2× throughput, ½ failure rate; 13-hr continuous espresso operation. (4 sources)
- [π0.6 / π0.6-MEM (intermediates)](entities/pi-zero-6.md) — anchor for the still-unpublished π-series generations between [π0.5](entities/pi-zero-5.md) and [π0.7](entities/pi07.md) / [π*0.6](entities/pistar06.md). Plain π0.5/π0.6 have no primary source; **π0.5-KI** is sourced via the [Knowledge Insulation paper](sources/knowledge-insulation-paper.md). (4 sources)
- [SmolVLA](entities/smolvla.md) — [Hugging Face](entities/hugging-face.md) LeRobot team's 450 M VLA (June 2025); SmolVLM-2 + flow-matching with interleaved CA + causal SA; 22.9 K community-dataset episodes; **beats π0-3.5 B by +16.6 pts on real-world SO-100 multi-task**; async-inference RobotClient/PolicyServer stack; runs on CPU (only frontier VLA that does). (12 sources)
- [OpenVLA](entities/openvla.md) — Kim et al. 2024; 7B open-weights baseline (Llama-2 backbone + autoregressive action tokens); the reference comparison VLA across nearly every 2024–2025 VLA paper. (8 sources)
- [VLA-0](entities/vla-0.md) — NVIDIA's **action-as-text** VLA (Goyal et al., Oct 2025); unmodified [Qwen2.5-VL-3B](entities/qwen.md) prints actions as integer strings — no head, no new tokens. Beats π0 / GR00T-N1 / OpenVLA-OFT / SmolVLA on LIBERO (avg 94.7) with **no action pretraining**; +12.5 pts over SmolVLA on real SO-100. Defines a "4th family" of VLA design. (1 source)
- [OpenVLA-OFT](entities/openvla-oft.md) — Kim/Finn/Liang (Stanford, RSS 2025) "Optimized Fine-Tuning" recipe on [OpenVLA](entities/openvla.md): **parallel decoding + action chunking + continuous L1 head** (+FiLM = OFT+). Lifts LIBERO **76.5 → 97.1** at **26× throughput** on the *same base weights* — the top LIBERO score in the wiki and the only pretrained model above [VLA-0](entities/vla-0.md); runs bimanual ALOHA at 25 Hz. (3 sources)
- [FAST / π0-FAST](entities/fast-action-tokenization.md) — [Physical Intelligence](entities/physical-intelligence.md)'s **Frequency-space Action Sequence Tokenization** ([Pertsch et al., RSS 2025](sources/fast-paper.md)): DCT + BPE compression of action chunks; π0-FAST = [π0](entities/pi-zero.md) + FAST tokens matches π0-diffusion at **5× less training**. Recurs as a discrete-token baseline *and* as the token scheme [Knowledge Insulation](concepts/learning/knowledge-insulation.md) uses inside π0.7 / π*0.6; [MolmoAct2](entities/molmoact2.md) ships an open-data reimplementation. (5 sources)
- [MolmoAct](entities/molmoact.md) — Allen Institute open "action reasoning" VLA (Lee et al. 2508.07917); discrete-token, reasons in space (depth tokens); LIBERO 86.8. **Superseded by [MolmoAct2](entities/molmoact2.md).** Also the pretrained policy **supervised by 11 frontier LLMs** in Anthropic's [robotics eval](sources/anthropic-how-claude-performs-on-robotics-tasks.md) — every one scored worse than MolmoAct alone in-distribution. (5 sources)
- [MolmoAct2](entities/molmoact2.md) — Ai2's **fully-open, deployable** VLA successor to MolmoAct; hybrid discrete+continuous head via [per-layer KV conditioning](concepts/learning/per-layer-kv-conditioning.md), [Molmo2-ER](entities/molmo2-er.md) backbone, [adaptive-depth](concepts/learning/adaptive-depth-reasoning.md) MolmoAct2-Think variant; **LIBERO 97.2 / Think 98.1 — top *tier*, [statistically tied](syntheses/platforms/vla-success-rate-audit.md) with OpenVLA-OFT / GR00T N1.7 / π0.5**; real YAM +15 pp over OpenVLA-OFT *does* survive; 55.8 Hz; <$6,000 setup. (3 sources)
- [Octo](entities/octo.md) — Octo Model Team 2024; transformer-from-scratch generalist policy trained on [OXE](entities/open-x-embodiment.md); pre-flow-matching-era baseline. (2 sources)
- [Gemini Robotics](entities/gemini-robotics.md) — Google DeepMind robot foundation models; full VLA + Gemini Robotics-**ER** embodied-reasoning VLM (tool-call planner); [1.5 report](sources/gemini-robotics-1-5-report.md) adds Motion Transfer + Embodied Thinking + agentic orchestration. (10 sources)
- [OK-Robot](entities/ok-robot.md) — NYU zero-shot pick-and-drop framework; 58.5% in 10 homes; 1.8× over OVMM. (2 sources)
- [Robot Utility Models](entities/robot-utility-models.md) — NYU/Meta zero-shot mobile-manipulation BC. (9 sources)
- [Dobb·E](entities/dobb-e.md) — NYU predecessor to RUM; HPR encoder + Stick-v1 + Homes of New York dataset. (4 sources)

### Behavior-cloning methods
- [DiffusionOPSD](entities/diffusionopsd.md) — reward post-training for diffusion/rectified-flow models as **on-policy self-distillation**: build an explicit bounded local target from reward gradients, fit it detached, rebuild it as the policy moves. Image generation only; listed here because the substrate is the same [flow-matching](concepts/learning/flow-matching.md) velocity field as the wiki's action heads. Ports the **diagnosis** and the clean-output identity `A₀ = A_τ − τ·v_θ`; **does not port** the reward gradient, since robot task rewards are rarely differentiable. (1 source)
- [Patch Policy](entities/patch-policy.md) — [LeCun](entities/yann-lecun.md) & [Pinto](entities/lerrel-pinto.md) (NYU, Jul 2026). A **block-causal attention mask** lets a small transformer policy consume **frozen** ViT patch tokens without a VLM backbone. Beats fine-tuned [OpenVLA-OFT](entities/openvla.md) in-domain at **51.55M vs 7.61B params** and **10.99 ms** latency (VQ-BeT head; the Diffusion Policy head is 445.85 ms). Backbone sweep: **WebSSL and DINOv2 win, SigLIP 2 and [V-JEPA 2](entities/v-jepa-2.md) lose**, and the ranking holds across action heads. (1 source)
- [DynaMo](entities/dynamo.md) — [Pinto](entities/lerrel-pinto.md) lab, NeurIPS 2024. **In-domain** SSL pretraining on your own demos: joint latent inverse + forward dynamics, no augmentations, no action labels, no out-of-domain data. **+39%**; works from 6 demonstrations. The counter-bet to frozen web-scale features — and **the same lab's [Patch Policy](entities/patch-policy.md) later beats it** with exactly that, though never in DynaMo's own low-demo regime. (2 sources)
- [IBC](entities/ibc.md) — Implicit Behavioral Cloning (Florence et al., CoRL 2021); energy-based-model BC; introduced PushT; direct ancestor of Diffusion Policy. (2 sources)
- [BET](entities/bet.md) — Behavior Transformer (Shafiullah et al., NeurIPS 2022); transformer + k-means action discretization; ancestor of VQ-BeT. (6 sources)
- [VQ-BeT](entities/vq-bet.md) — Vector-Quantized Behavior Transformer (Lee et al. 2024); top performer in RUM ablation; LeRobot-supported. (12 sources)
- [Diffusion Policy](entities/diffusion-policy.md) — Chi et al. 2023, Columbia/TRI/MIT; conditional DDPM over actions; popularized PushT + UMI gripper + action-chunking convention; LeRobot-supported (263 M params; CPU-incompatible without acceleration); single-task ancestor of TRI [LBMs](concepts/learning/large-behavior-models.md); now commercialized by [Walden Robotics](entities/walden-robotics.md). (30 sources)
- [ACT (Action Chunking Transformer)](entities/act.md) — Zhao et al. 2023, Stanford; default IL policy for [ALOHA / Mobile ALOHA](entities/aloha.md); operationalized **action chunking** as a first-class IL primitive (now near-default across 2024–2026 BC + VLA); LeRobot's most-used policy (52 M params, ~100–200 Hz on RTX 4090). (16 sources)
- [ACT++](entities/act-plus-plus.md) — the mobile-extended ML codebase shipped with [Mobile ALOHA](entities/aloha.md) (`MarkFzp/act-plus-plus`); adds the 16-dim base+arm action vector + co-training + action-chunk delay-shift. (2 sources)
- [UMI](entities/umi.md) — Universal Manipulation Interface (Chi et al., RSS 2024); hand-held gripper data-collection system; same lead author as Diffusion Policy; 32 h in the [TRI LBM](sources/tri-lbm-paper.md) pretraining mix. (3 sources)

### Real-world RL methods / systems
- [SAC](entities/sac.md) — Soft Actor-Critic (Haarnoja, Zhou, Abbeel, Levine; ICML 2018 + 1812.05905 practical follow-up w/ automatic temperature α + real-robot demos); max-entropy off-policy actor-critic; sample-efficient + seed-stable; algorithmic root of the RLPD/SERL lineage. (3 sources)
- [RLPD](entities/rlpd.md) — Reinforcement Learning with Prior Data (Ball, Smith, Kostrikov, Levine; ICML 2023); off-policy SAC recipe = symmetric sampling + critic LayerNorm + ensembles; ~2.5× over prior offline-to-online. Base algorithm of the SERL family. (6 sources)
